Private Const col_no_ContractType = 70  'Edit 11-07-2024 (05-Nov-2024 69->70)
Private Const col_no_ChargeBack_ContractType_Inc_Exc = 71 '(05-Nov-2024 70->71)
Private Const col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementOption = 72  'Edit 11-07-2024 (23-Sep-2024 70->71) (05-Nov-2024 71->72)
Private Const col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementPlant = 73  'Edit 11-07-2024 (23-Sep-2024 71->72) (05-Nov-2024 72->73)
Private Const col_no_PaymentTerms = 74 'Edit 11-07-2024 (23-Sep-2024 72->73) (05-Nov-2024 73->74)
Private Const col_no_PaymentMethod = 75  'Edit 11-07-2024 (23-Sep-2024 73->74) (05-Nov-2024 74->75)
Private Const col_no_ConditionTable = 76  'Edit 11-07-2024 (23-Sep-2024 74->75) (05-Nov-2024 75->76)
Private Const col_no_FieldCombination = 77  'Edit 11-07-2024 (23-Sep-2024 75->76) (05-Nov-2024 76->77)
Private Const col_no_IM_Reject = 78  'Edit 11-07-2024 (23-Sep-2024 76->77) (05-Nov-2024 77->78)
Private Const col_no_Online_EN = "138" 'Edit 11-07-2024 'Not found on new template
Private Const col_no_Online_TH = "139" 'Edit 11-07-2024 'Not found on new template
Private Const col_no_ConvertStatus = 149 'Edit 11-07-2024 (23-Sep-2024 150->148) (05-Nov-2024 148->149)

Private Const cell_addr_Theme = "C4"
Private Const cell_addr_Prepared_by = "C12"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_start_day = "C7"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_start_month = "D7"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_start_year = "E7"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_end_day = "G7"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_end_month = "H7"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_end_year = "I7"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_Name = "M4"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_start_Time = "C9"
Private Const cell_addr_Promo_end_Time = "F9"
Private Const cell_addr_WBS_no = "M9"
Private Const cell_addr_All_Day = "M7"
Private Const cell_addr_Mon = "N7"
Private Const cell_addr_Tue = "O7"
Private Const cell_addr_Wed = "P7"
Private Const cell_addr_Thu = "Q7"
Private Const cell_addr_Fri = "R7"
Private Const cell_addr_Sat = "S7"
Private Const cell_addr_Sun = "T7"

Private Const server_name = "M90SVWPRPAD1"
Private Const database_name = "RPA_PRD_DB"

Private Const path_dir_tmp = "C:\Temp_STD\"
Private Const path_SFTP_approved As String = "\\10.90.230.41\RPA\PRD\bby\MER_C_UPLOAD_TO_PORTAL\APPROVED\"
Private Const path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template = "\\10.90.230.41\rpa\PRD\bbyc\Mer C\00_Mer C Template\Promotion BBY\Convert_C_to_AB_Template_V2.xlsb"

Dim tmp_path_C2AB_Template As String
Dim Prepared_by As String

Dim srcPath_file As String
Dim fso As Object
Dim path_dir_output_tmp As String

Dim wb_MerC As Workbook
Dim sheet_MerC As Worksheet
Private Const sheet_name_Mer_C_Template = "Mer C Template"

'=======================================
'C2AB = Convert_C_to_AB_Template_V2.xlsb
'=======================================
Private Const C2AB_sheet_name_BBY = "Promotion BBY"
Private Const C2AB_sheet_name_STD = "(2)Create-Data"
Private Const C2AB_sheet_name_Mechanic = "Mechanic"
Private Const C2AB_sheet_name_Mat_Group = "Material Grouping"
Private Const C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template = "Mer_C_Template"

'----------------------------
'SubCon = Sub_condition sheet
'----------------------------
Private Const C2AB_SubCon_col_letter_PlantSaleOrg = "DV"
Private Const C2AB_SubCon_col_letter_SaleOrg = "DW"
Private Const C2AB_SubCon_row_start_SaleOrg = 23
Private Const C2AB_SubCon_row_end_SaleOrg = 29

Sub Convert_to_STD_Link(path_file_MerC As String)
    set_Application False

    Set wb_MerC = Workbooks.Open(path_file_MerC)
    Set sheet_MerC = Sheets(sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Prepared_by = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Prepared_by).Value2
    If InStr(Prepared_by, "@") > 0 And Len(Trim(Prepared_by)) > 0 Then
        Prepared_by = Left(Prepared_by, InStr(Prepared_by, "@") - 1)
        Prepared_by = Replace(Prepared_by, ".", "")
        Prepared_by = Replace(Prepared_by, "_", "")
    Else
        MsgBox ("Please input prepared by")
        Exit Sub
    End If

    srcPath_file = path_file_MerC
    path_dir_output_tmp = Create_Folder
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    tmp_path_C2AB_Template = path_dir_output_tmp & fso.GetFileName(path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template)
    Call Search_Promotion()
    set_Application True
End Sub

Sub set_Application(is_active As Boolean)
    Application.ScreenUpdating = is_active
    Application.DisplayStatusBar = is_active
    Application.DisplayAlerts = is_active
End Sub

Private Sub Search_Promotion()
    sheet_MerC.Activate

    Dim last_row As Long
    Dim last_col As Long
    last_row = Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row 'Find the last non-blank cell in column Material number
    last_col = Cells(CInt(row_start), Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column 'Find the last non-blank cell in row 16

    Dim filename_Convert_to_STD As String
    Dim Profile_type As String
    Dim Mechanic As String
    Dim Promotion_Name As String
    Dim Flow_Type As String
    Dim Online_EN As String
    Dim Online_TH As String
    Dim WBS As String
    Dim Promo_start_time As String
    Dim Promo_end_time As String
    Dim All_day As String
    Dim Mon As String
    Dim Tue As String
    Dim Wed As String
    Dim Thu As String
    Dim Fri As String
    Dim Sat As String
    Dim Sun As String
    Dim Theme As String
    Dim Contract_Type As String
    Dim Promo_start_date As String
    Dim Promo_end_date As String
    Dim Promo_start_datetime As Date
    Dim Promo_end_datetime As Date
    Dim Rebate_Reason As String 'Add 11-07-2024

    If Application.International(xlDateOrder) = 0 Then 'Month-Day-Year
        Promo_start_datetime = CDate(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value & " 23:59:59")
        Promo_end_datetime = CDate(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_month).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_day).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_year).Value & " 23:59:59")
    ElseIf Application.International(xlDateOrder) = 1 Then 'Day-Month_year
        Promo_start_datetime = CDate(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value & " 23:59:59")
        Promo_end_datetime = CDate(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_day).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_month).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_year).Value & " 23:59:59")
    End If

    Dim Promo_start_year As Integer
    Dim Promo_end_year As Integer
    Promo_start_year = Year(Promo_start_datetime)
    Promo_end_year = Year(Promo_end_datetime)
 
    If Promo_start_year > 2500 Then
        Promo_start_datetime = DateAdd("yyyy", -543, Promo_start_datetime)
    End If

    If Promo_end_year > 2500 Then
        Promo_end_datetime = DateAdd("yyyy", -543, Promo_end_datetime)
    End If

    Promo_start_date = Format(Promo_start_datetime, "DD.MM.YYYY")
    Promo_end_date = Format(Promo_end_datetime, "DD.MM.YYYY")
    Promotion_Name = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_Name).Value2
    WBS = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_WBS_no).Value2
    Promo_start_time = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_Time).Value2
    Promo_end_time = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_Time).Value2
    All_day = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_All_Day).Value2
    Mon = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Mon).Value2
    Tue = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Tue).Value2
    Wed = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Wed).Value2
    Thu = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Thu).Value2
    Fri = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Fri).Value2
    Sat = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Sat).Value2
    Sun = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Sun).Value2
    Theme = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Theme).Value2
    
    Dim row_no_firstPromoItem As Integer
    Dim row_no_lastPromoItem As Integer
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    Dim Promo_no As String
    Dim is_All_Skip As Boolean
    Dim sub_curr_row_no As Integer
    Dim sub_curr_row_no_2 As Integer

    Dim curr_Promo_no As Integer
    
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To last_row
        sheet_MerC.Activate
        If sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2 <> "" Then
            If curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) Then
                Promo_no = sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2
                row_no_firstPromoItem = curr_row_no

                If row_start = last_row Then
                    Call set_RebateReason_n_ContractType(Rebate_Reason, Contract_Type, row_no_firstPromoItem)
                    Call set_row_no_lastPromoItem(row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem)

                    For sub_curr_row_no = row_no_firstPromoItem To row_no_lastPromoItem
                        Mechanic = sheet_MerC.Cells(sub_curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                        If Mechanic = "B1G1 (On Pack)" Or Mechanic = "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                            is_All_Skip = True
                        Else
                            If Len(Trim(sheet_MerC.Cells(sub_curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_IM_Reject)).Value2)) = 0 Then
                                If sheet_MerC.Cells(sub_curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ConvertStatus)).Value2 = "Done" Then
                                    is_All_Skip = True
                                Else
                                    is_All_Skip = False
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                            Else
                                is_All_Skip = True
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next sub_curr_row_no

                    If is_All_Skip = False Then
                        fso.CopyFile path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template, tmp_path_C2AB_Template, True
                        Call set_row_no_lastPromoItem(row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem)
                        Call Copy_Mer_C_Data(Promo_start_datetime, Promo_end_datetime, Theme, Promotion_Name, Online_EN, Online_TH, WBS, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, All_day, Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun, Contract_Type, row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem, Rebate_Reason)
                        sheet_MerC.Activate
    
                        For sub_curr_row_no_2 = row_no_firstPromoItem To row_no_lastPromoItem
                            sheet_MerC.Cells(sub_curr_row_no_2, CInt(col_no_ConvertStatus)).Value2 = "Done"
                        Next sub_curr_row_no_2
                        
                        Application.DisplayAlerts = False
                        wb_MerC.Save
                        Application.DisplayAlerts = True
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                curr_Promo_no = sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2
                If Len(Trim(curr_Promo_no)) > 0 Then
                    If Promo_no <> curr_Promo_no Or curr_row_no = last_row Then
                        If curr_row_no = last_row Then
                            If Promo_no = curr_Promo_no Then
                                row_no_lastPromoItem = curr_row_no
                            Else
                                If sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no - 1, CInt(col_no_ConvertStatus)).Value2 <> "Done" Then ' Case Last Promotion has one Material
                                    curr_row_no = curr_row_no - 1
                                Else
                                    row_no_firstPromoItem = curr_row_no
                                    row_no_lastPromoItem = curr_row_no
                                    Promo_no = curr_Promo_no
                                End If
                            End If
                        ElseIf Promo_no <> curr_Promo_no Then
                            Call set_row_no_lastPromoItem(row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem)
                        End If
                        
                        Call set_RebateReason_n_ContractType(Rebate_Reason, Contract_Type, row_no_firstPromoItem)
                        Call set_row_no_lastPromoItem(row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem)
                        
                        For sub_curr_row_no = row_no_firstPromoItem To row_no_lastPromoItem
                            Mechanic = sheet_MerC.Cells(sub_curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                            If Mechanic = "B1G1 (On Pack)" Or Mechanic = "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                is_All_Skip = True
                            Else
                                is_All_Skip = False
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next sub_curr_row_no
                        ' Check Already Convert
                        If is_All_Skip = False Then
                          
                        End If
                        
                        If is_All_Skip = False Then
                              
                              For sub_curr_row_no = row_no_firstPromoItem To row_no_lastPromoItem
                                If sheet_MerC.Cells(sub_curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ConvertStatus)).Value2 = "Done" Then
                                    is_All_Skip = True
                                Else
                                    is_All_Skip = False
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                            Next sub_curr_row_no
                            If is_All_Skip = False Then
                                fso.CopyFile path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template, tmp_path_C2AB_Template, True
                                Call set_row_no_lastPromoItem(row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem)
                                Call Copy_Mer_C_Data(Promo_start_datetime, Promo_end_datetime, Theme, Promotion_Name, Online_EN, Online_TH, WBS, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, All_day, Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun, Contract_Type, row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem, Rebate_Reason)
                                sheet_MerC.Activate

                                For sub_curr_row_no_2 = row_no_firstPromoItem To row_no_lastPromoItem
                                    sheet_MerC.Cells(sub_curr_row_no_2, CInt(col_no_ConvertStatus)).Value2 = "Done"
                                Next sub_curr_row_no_2
                                Application.DisplayAlerts = False
                                wb_MerC.Save
                                Application.DisplayAlerts = True
                            End If
                        End If
                        
                        If curr_row_no = last_row And Promo_no <> sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2 Then
                            curr_row_no = curr_row_no - 1
                        End If
                        
                        row_no_firstPromoItem = curr_row_no
                        If Len(Trim(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2)) = 0 Then
                            row_no_firstPromoItem = curr_row_no + 1
                            Promo_no = sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2
                        Else
                            row_no_firstPromoItem = curr_row_no
                            Promo_no = sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2
                        End If
                    Else
                        row_no_lastPromoItem = curr_row_no
                    End If
                Else
                    row_no_lastPromoItem = curr_row_no
                End If
            End If
        Else
            row_no_lastPromoItem = curr_row_no - 1
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    
    'Recheck Not Convert
    Promo_no = 0
    row_no_firstPromoItem = 0
    row_no_lastPromoItem = 0
    
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To last_row
        If sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ConvertStatus)).Value2 = "Done" Then
        Else
            If Promo_no = 0 Then
                Promo_no = sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2
                row_no_firstPromoItem = curr_row_no
            ElseIf Promo_no <> sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Number_of_Promotion)).Value2 Then
                row_no_lastPromoItem = curr_row_no
            End If
            Mechanic = sheet_MerC.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
            If Mechanic = "B1G1 (On Pack)" Or Mechanic = "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                is_All_Skip = True
                Promo_no = 0
                row_no_firstPromoItem = 0
                row_no_lastPromoItem = 0
            Else
                is_All_Skip = False
            End If
            If is_All_Skip = False Then
                Call set_row_no_lastPromoItem(row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem)

                fso.CopyFile path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template, tmp_path_C2AB_Template, True
                If Left(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ContractType).Value2, 2) = "Z2" Then
                    Contract_Type = sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ContractType).Value2
                    Rebate_Reason = Left(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ChargeBack_Reason).Value2, 1) 'Add 11-07-2024
                Else
                    Contract_Type = ""
                    Rebate_Reason = "0" + Left(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ChargeBack_Reason).Value2, 1) 'Add 11-07-2024
                End If
                Call Copy_Mer_C_Data(Promo_start_datetime, Promo_end_datetime, Theme, Promotion_Name, Online_EN, Online_TH, WBS, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, All_day, Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun, Contract_Type, row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem, Rebate_Reason)
                sheet_MerC.Activate

                For sub_curr_row_no_2 = row_no_firstPromoItem To row_no_lastPromoItem
                    sheet_MerC.Cells(sub_curr_row_no_2, CInt(col_no_ConvertStatus)).Value2 = "Done"
                Next sub_curr_row_no_2
                Application.DisplayAlerts = False
                wb_MerC.Save
                Application.DisplayAlerts = True
            End If
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    
    Dim strFileName As String
    Dim strFileExists As String

On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    Kill tmp_path_C2AB_Template
                  
    MsgBox ("Convert to standard complete")
    Exit Sub
ErrorHandler:
    MsgBox ("Convert to standard complete")

    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub set_RebateReason_n_ContractType(ByRef Rebate_Reason As String, ByRef Contract_Type As String, row_no_firstPromoItem As Integer)
    If Left(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ContractType).Value2, 2) = "Z2" Then
        Contract_Type = sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ContractType).Value2
        Rebate_Reason = Left(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ChargeBack_Reason).Value2, 1) 'Add 11-07-2024
    Else
        Contract_Type = ""
        If Left(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ChargeBack_Reason).Value2, 1) <> "A" Then
            Rebate_Reason = "0" + Left(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ChargeBack_Reason).Value2, 1) 'Add 11-07-2024
        Else
            Rebate_Reason = Left(sheet_MerC.Cells(row_no_firstPromoItem, col_no_ChargeBack_Reason).Value2, 1)
        End If
    End If
End Sub

Sub set_row_no_lastPromoItem(row_no_firstPromoItem As Integer, ByRef row_no_lastPromoItem As Integer)
    If row_no_firstPromoItem > row_no_lastPromoItem Then
        row_no_lastPromoItem = row_no_firstPromoItem
    End If
End Sub

Private Function Check_Reject(c_lRow As Long, std_C_Sheet As Worksheet) As Boolean
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    Dim is_Reject As Boolean
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_IM_Reject)).Value2 = "Reject" Then
            is_Reject = True
            Exit For
        Else
            is_Reject = False
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    Check_Reject = is_Reject
End Function

Private Function Check_Reject_Row(Row_index As Integer, std_C_Sheet As Worksheet) As Boolean
    Dim is_Reject As Boolean
    If std_C_Sheet.Cells(Row_index, CInt(col_no_IM_Reject)).Value2 = "Reject" Then
        is_Reject = True
    Else
        is_Reject = False
    End If
    Check_Reject_Row = is_Reject
End Function

Private Function Check_All_Reject(c_lRow As Long, std_C_Sheet As Worksheet) As Boolean
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    Dim is_Reject As Boolean
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_IM_Reject)).Value2 = "Reject" Then
            is_Reject = True
        Else
            is_Reject = False
            Exit For
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    Check_All_Reject = is_Reject
End Function

Private Sub Copy_Mer_C_Data(Promo_start_date As Date, Promo_end_date As Date, Theme As String, Promotion_Name As String, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, WBS As String, Promo_start_time As String, Promo_end_time As String, All_day As String, Mon As String, Tue As String, Wed As String, Thu As String, Fri As String, Sat As String, Sun As String, Contract_Type As String, row_no_firstPromoItem As Integer, row_no_lastPromoItem As Integer, Rebate_Reason As String) 'Add Rebate Reason 11-07-2024
    Dim Start_Day As String, Start_Month As String, Promo_start_year As String
    Dim End_Day As String, End_Month As String, Promo_end_year As String

    'Dim path_file_MerC As String
    'Set sheet_MerC = Sheets("Convert File")
    'path_file_MerC = sheet_MerC.Range("D3").Value

    Dim sheet_name_Mer_C_Template As String
    
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Dim Mat_Group_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    Set std_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_STD)
    Set Sub_Con_Sheet = Sheets("Sub_condition")
    Set Mat_Group_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mat_Group)
    Set Profile_Master_Sheet = Sheets("ZRT0013_10 updated 11-Nov")
    std_C_Sheet.Activate

    Dim Vendor_Code As String

        Vendor_Code = "NOBP"
    End If
    
    Dim c_lRow As Long
    Dim C_lCol As Long
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    c_lRow = Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row 'Find the last non-blank cell in column Z
    C_lCol = Cells(CInt(row_start), Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column 'Find the last non-blank cell in row 16
    Dim is_Compensate As Boolean, is_DC As Boolean, is_Promo_tag As Boolean, is_Mat_Group As Boolean, isZ3 As Boolean, Online_Only As Boolean

    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ContractType)).Value2)) > 0 Then
            If Left(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ContractType)).Value2, 2) = "Z2" Then
                is_Compensate = True
                Exit For
            ElseIf Left(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ContractType)).Value2, 2) = "Z3" Then
                isZ3 = True
                is_Compensate = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Else
            isZ3 = False
            is_Compensate = False
        End If
    Next curr_row_no

        is_DC = False

    Else
       is_Online_Material = False
    End If

         If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(g, CInt(col_no_PromoTag)).Value2)) > 0 Then
            is_Promo_tag = True

         End If
    Next g

    'Look up master For Theme

            Theme = Sub_Con_Sheet.Range("J" & t).Value2
            Exit For
        End If

    Dim Mat_Group_Name_List() As String

        Application.DisplayAlerts = False

    Promo_start_year = IIf(Year(Promo_start_date) < 2000, Year(Promo_start_date) + 543, Year(Promo_start_date))
    End_Day = Day(Promo_end_date)
    End_Month = Month(Promo_end_date)
    Promo_end_year = IIf(Year(Promo_end_date) < 2000, Year(Promo_end_date) + 543, Year(Promo_end_date))
    Start_Day_Fill = Right("0" & Start_Day, 2) & "." & Right("0" & Start_Month, 2) & "." & Promo_start_year
    End_Day_Fill = Right("0" & End_Day, 2) & "." & Right("0" & End_Month, 2) & "." & Promo_end_year
  ' Dim Mat_Group_Desc As String
    Mat_Group_Index = 4
    If is_Mat_Group = True Then
        For m = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
            std_C_Sheet.Activate
            If std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2 <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2 <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                Mat_Group_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2
                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                MCH_LV2 = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_MCH_LV2)).Value2
                ' Mat_Group_Desc = Promotion_Name & "_" & Mat_Group_Number
                Mat_Group_Sheet.Activate
                Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(Mat_Group_Index, 1).Value2 = Mat_Group_Number
                Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(Mat_Group_Index, 5).Value2 = Material_Number
                Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(Mat_Group_Index, 6).Value2 = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(Mat_Group_Index, 4).Value2 = "1 - Material No."
                Mat_Group_Index = Mat_Group_Index + 1
            End If
        Next m
        Dim M_lRow As Integer
        Dim M_group_Number As String
        Dim M_group_Name As String
        Dim M_Group_Number_STR As String
        Dim M_Group_Name_STR As String
        Dim M_Index As Integer
        Dim M_Number_Index As Integer
        Dim Get_Item_Index As Integer
        
        M_Index = 0
        M_Number_Index = 0
        Mat_Group_Sheet.Activate
        M_lRow = Cells(Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
        For m = 4 To M_lRow
            M_group_Name = Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(m, 2).Value2
            M_group_Number = Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(m, 1).Value2
            M_Group_Name_STR = Join(Mat_Group_Name_List, ",")
            M_Group_Number_STR = Join(Mat_Group_Number_List, ",")
            
            If InStr(M_Group_Name_STR, M_group_Name) > 0 Then
            Else
                If Len(Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(m, 2).Value2) > 0 Then
                    Mat_Group_Name_List(M_Index) = M_group_Name
                    M_Index = M_Index + 1
                    ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Name_List(M_Index)
                End If
            End If
            If InStr(M_Group_Number_STR, M_group_Number) > 0 Then
            Else
                If Len(Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(m, 1).Value2) > 0 Then
                    Mat_Group_Number_List(M_Number_Index) = M_group_Number
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                    ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Number_List(M_Number_Index)
                End If
            End If
        Next m
        'Mat_Group_Number_List = GetUniqueValuesFromColumnIntoArray(Mat_Group_Sheet, "A")
        'Mat_Group_Name_List = GetUniqueValuesFromColumnIntoArray(Mat_Group_Sheet, "B")
        ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Name_List(M_Index - 1)
        ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Number_List(M_Number_Index - 1)
    Else
        ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Name_List(0)
        ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Number_List(0)
    End If

    Date_Now = Now()
    Dim Year_Now As Integer
    Year_Now = CInt(Format(Now(), "yyyy"))
    If Year_Now > 2500 Then
        Date_Now = DateAdd("yyyy", -543, Date_Now)
    End If
        
    If is_DC = True Then
        Bonus_Buy_Index = 1
        
        Promo_start_date = DateAdd("d", -7, Promo_start_date)
        
        
        If Promo_start_date < Date_Now Then
            If Application.International(xlDateOrder) = 0 Then 'Month-Day-Year
                Promo_start_date = CDate(Month(Date_Now) & "/" & Day(Date_Now) & "/" & IIf(Year(Date_Now) < 2000, Year(Date_Now) + 543, Year(Date_Now)))
            ElseIf Application.International(xlDateOrder) = 1 Then 'Day-Month_year
                Promo_start_date = CDate(Day(Date_Now) & "/" & Month(Date_Now) & "/" & IIf(Year(Date_Now) < 2000, Year(Date_Now) + 543, Year(Date_Now)))
            End If
        End If
        
        Start_Day = Day(Promo_start_date)
        Start_Month = Month(Promo_start_date)

        Start_Day_Fill = Right("0" & Start_Day, 2) & "." & Right("0" & Start_Month, 2) & "." & Promo_start_year

        If Len(Contract_Type) > 0 Then 'DC No contract
            is_Contract = True
            BBY_Sheet.Activate
            'BBY_Sheet.Range("C12").Value2 = "N"

        Else

            'BBY_Sheet.Range("C12").Value2 = "N"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("C12").Value2 = "03" ' Confirm from meeting 05-08-2024
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E12").Value2 = ""
            is_Contract = False
        End If
        
        BBY_STD_Data_index = 14
        '-------------------GEN DC FILE--------------------------
        Call Gen_DC_File(Plant, CStr(Promo_start_year), CStr(Start_Month), CStr(Start_Day), Purchasing_Group, Profile_type, Mat_Group_Number_List, Mat_Group_Name_List, Vendor_Code, is_Contract)

        fso.CopyFile path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template, tmp_path_C2AB_Template, True

        Promo_start_date = CDate(CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value2) & "-" & CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value2) & "-" & CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value2))

'                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                                    Else
'                                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                                    End If
                                    '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                        '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Else
                                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                        Else
                                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                            Else
                                                '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                End If
                                Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2

                                    End If

                                Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, True, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1

                    Else

                Else
                    Have_Non_Promo_Tag = True

                                    End If
                                Else

                                    std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index

                                    If is_Fill = False Then

                                            Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                        Else
                                            Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                        End If
                                        If isZ3 = True Then

                                        End If
                                        is_Fill = True
                                    End If
                                End If
                                
                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                    Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2

                                    Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                                    New_Price = ""

                                        Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2

                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                    BBY_Check = BBY_Check + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If

            End If

                                    Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                    Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                    Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                    If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                        If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                                            Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                            Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                        Else
                                            Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                            Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                            Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next curr_row_no
                Else
                    M_Number_Index = 0
                    Set BBY_Sheet = Worksheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
                    For Each M_Number In Mat_Group_Number_List
                        For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                            If M_Number = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2) Then
                                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                                
                                Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                                Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                                New_Price = ""
                                Discount_Amount = ""
                                Discount_Percent = ""
                                If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                Else
'                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                                    Else
'                                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                                    End If
                                    '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                        '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Else
                                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                        Else
                                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                            Else
                                                '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                End If
                                Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                                Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                                Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                                'Exit For
                            End If
                        Next curr_row_no
                        BBY_Sheet.Activate
                        If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                            Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    New_Price = ""
                                    Discount_Percent = ""
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If

                                Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call D002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call D003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                        Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                    Else
                                        Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                    End If
                                    Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then

                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call F002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call F003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            End If
                            If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                            End If
                        Else

                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then

                                    Discount_Percent = ""
                                End If
                                Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1

                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                                Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                                Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                                Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                                Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            End If
                            If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next M_Number
                End If
            Else
                Save_File_Name = path_dir_output_tmp & CStr(Promo_start_year) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Month), 2) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Day), 2) & "_" & Left(Profile_type, 4) & "_C_BBY_" & Left(Trim(Purchasing_Group), 3) & "_" & Vendor_Code & "_" & Format(Date_Now, "yyyymmdd") & "_" & Format(Now(), "hhmmss") & "_" & UCase(Prepared_by) & ".xlsx"
                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                Plant_List = GetPlant(CInt(row_start))
                Plant_Str = Join(Plant_List, ",")
                Call Mark_Plant(Plant_List)
                
                If is_Mat_Group = False Then
                    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                        is_Reject_Row = Check_Reject_Row(curr_row_no, std_C_Sheet)
                        If is_Reject_Row = False Then
                            Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                            
                            Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                            Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                            New_Price = ""
                            Discount_Amount = ""
                            Discount_Percent = ""
                            If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                                New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                            Else

'                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                                Else
'                                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                                End If
                                '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                    '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                Else
                                    If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Else
                                        If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                            Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                        Else
                                            '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                            Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                End If

                            End If
                            Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                            Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                            Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                            If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                                WBS = std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_WBS_no).Value2
                                Promo_start_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("C9").Value2
                                Promo_end_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("F9").Value2
                                
                                Current_Bonus_Buy_Number = Bonus_Buy_Index

                                    Else

                                    End If

                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                            Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                            Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                            Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                            Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        End If

                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                            Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                            Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                            Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                            Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)

                                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index

                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                    Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                    Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                    Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                    Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                    If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                        Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                        Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                        Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next curr_row_no
                Else
                    M_Number_Index = 0
                    For Each M_Number In Mat_Group_Number_List
                        For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                            If M_Number = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2) Then
                                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                                
                                Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                                Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                                New_Price = ""
                                Discount_Amount = ""
                                Discount_Percent = ""
                                If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                Else

'                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                                    Else
'                                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                                    End If
                                    '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                        '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Else
                                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                        Else
                                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                            Else
                                                '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                End If
                                Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                                Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                                Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                            End If
                        Next curr_row_no
                        
                        If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                            Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    New_Price = ""
                                    Discount_Percent = ""
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call D002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call D002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call D003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call D003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                        Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                    Else
                                        Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                    End If
                                    If isZ3 = True Then
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    Else
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    End If
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call F002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call F002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call F003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call F003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            End If
                            If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                            End If
                        Else

                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)

                                Else
                                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                                Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                                Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1

                                    Else
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    End If
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                                Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then

                                End If

                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then

                                If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
