                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next M_Number
                End If
            End If
            
            If Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P011" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P001" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P015" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P103" Then
                Call Fill_Card_Type
            End If
            
            BBY_Sheet.Activate
            '-------------------FILL CONTRACT------------------------
            If is_Compensate = True Then
                Call Fill_Contract
            End If
            
            activebookname = ActiveWorkbook.Name
            Application.Run "Convert_C_to_AB_Template_V2.xlsb!Save_File_MER_C", Save_File_Name
            Call Add_Data_to_SQL(tmp_path_C2AB_Template, Save_File_Name)
            Call Copy_File_To_Approve_Folder(Save_File_Name)
            'Call Workbooks(activebookname).Application.Run("!Save_File_MER_C,", DC_File_Name)
            '-------------------------------------------
            'Dim Save_book As Workbook
            Set Save_book = Workbooks.Open(Save_File_Name)
            Application.DisplayAlerts = False
            Save_book.Close
            Application.DisplayAlerts = True
            If is_Online_Material = True And InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") = 0 Then
                fso.CopyFile path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template, tmp_path_C2AB_Template, True
                Call Generate_Online_File(Promo_start_date, Promo_end_date, Theme, Promotion_Name, Online_EN, Online_TH, WBS, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, All_day, Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun, Contract_Type, row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem, Rebate_Reason)

        End If
  '-----------NO DC Generate Normal---------------------------------------------------------
    Else 'No DC Generate Normal
        BBY_Sheet.Activate

        Bonus_Buy_Index = 1
        Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14
        Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = 1

        Start_Day_Fill = Right("0" & Start_Day, 2) & "." & Right("0" & Start_Month, 2) & "." & Promo_start_year

        Call Fill_BBY_Header(Promotion_Name, Purchasing_Group, Theme, Profile_type, Contract_Type, Vendor_Code, Start_Day_Fill, End_Day_Fill, Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun, All_day, Rebate_Reason)

                                Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2

                                    Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2

                                End If

                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                BBY_Check = BBY_Check + 1
                                
                            End If
                        Else
                            Have_non_Compensate = True
                        End If
                    Else
                        Have_Non_Promo_Tag = True
                    End If
                End If

                            Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                            
                            If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                                WBS = std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_WBS_no).Value2
                                Promo_start_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("C9").Value2
                                Promo_end_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("F9").Value2
                                
                                Current_Bonus_Buy_Number = Bonus_Buy_Index
                                BBY_Sheet.Activate
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                        Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                    Else
                                        Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                    End If
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                        Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                        Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                        Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                        Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P103" Then
                                        Call P103_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                            If (Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerQty_SAP)).Value2)) = 0 And Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Compensate_Percent)).Value2)) = 0) Or isZ3 = True Then
                                Have_non_Compensate = True
                            Else
                                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                                BBY_Sheet.Activate
                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                    Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                    Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                    Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                    Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                    If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                        If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                                            Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                            Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                        Else
                                            Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                            Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                            Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P103" Then
                                    Call Gen_P103(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next curr_row_no
                Else
                    For Each M_Number In Mat_Group_Number_List
                        For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                            If M_Number = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2) Then
                                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                                
                                Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                                Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                                New_Price = ""
                                Discount_Amount = ""
                                Discount_Percent = ""
                                If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                Else
'                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                                    Else
'                                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                                    End If
                                    '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                        '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Else
                                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                        Else
                                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                            Else
                                                '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                End If
                                Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                                Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                                Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                            End If
                            
                        Next curr_row_no
                        If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                            Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    New_Price = ""
                                    Discount_Percent = ""
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call D002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call D003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                        Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                    Else
                                        Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                    End If
                                    Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call F002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                Call F003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                            If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                            End If
                        Else
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then

                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then

                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    New_Price = ""
                                    Discount_Percent = ""
                                End If
                                Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                                Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                                Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                                Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                                Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                            Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                        End If
                        If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                            M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                        End If
                    Next M_Number
                End If
            Else 'No Promotag No Compensate
                Save_File_Name = path_dir_output_tmp & CStr(Promo_start_year) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Month), 2) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Day), 2) & "_" & Left(Profile_type, 4) & "_C_BBY_" & Left(Trim(Purchasing_Group), 3) & "_" & Vendor_Code & "_" & Format(Date_Now, "yyyymmdd") & "_" & Format(Now(), "hhmmss") & "_" & UCase(Prepared_by) & ".xlsx"
                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                Plant_List = GetPlant(CInt(row_start))
                Plant_Str = Join(Plant_List, ",")
                Call Mark_Plant(Plant_List)

                If is_Mat_Group = False Then
                    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                        is_Reject_Row = Check_Reject_Row(curr_row_no, std_C_Sheet)
                        If is_Reject_Row = False Then
                            Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                            
                            Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                            Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                            New_Price = ""
                            Discount_Amount = ""
                            Discount_Percent = ""
                            If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                                New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                            Else
'                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                                Else
'                                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                                End If
                                '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                    '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                Else
                                    If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Else
                                        If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                            Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                        Else
                                            '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                            Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                End If

                            End If
                            Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                            Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                            Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                            If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                                WBS = std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_WBS_no).Value2
                                Promo_start_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("C9").Value2
                                Promo_end_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("F9").Value2
                                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                                
                                Current_Bonus_Buy_Number = Bonus_Buy_Index
                                
                                BBY_Sheet.Activate
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then

                                    Else
                                        Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                    End If
                                    If isZ3 = True Then

                                            Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                            Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                            Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                            Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                            Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P103" Then
                                            Call P103_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                            Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                            Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                            Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                            Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                            Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P103" Then
                                            Call P103_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                            If (Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerQty_SAP)).Value2)) = 0 And Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Compensate_Percent)).Value2)) = 0) Or isZ3 = True Then
                                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                                BBY_Sheet.Activate

                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                    Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                    Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                    Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                    Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                    If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                        If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                                            Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                            Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                        Else
                                            If isZ3 = True Then
                                                Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                            Else
                                                Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                            End If
                                            
                                            Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                            Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P103" Then
                                    Call Gen_P103(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next curr_row_no
                Else
                    M_Number_Index = 0
                    For Each M_Number In Mat_Group_Number_List
                        For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                            If M_Number = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2) Then
                                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                                
                                Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                                Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                                New_Price = ""
                                Discount_Amount = ""
                                Discount_Percent = ""
                                If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                Else

'                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                                    Else
'                                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                                    End If
                                    '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                        '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                    Else
                                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                        Else
                                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                            Else
                                                '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                End If
                                Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                                Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                                Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                            End If
                        Next curr_row_no
                        If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                            Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Promotion_Area = "P1"
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    New_Price = ""
                                    Discount_Percent = ""
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call D002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call D002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call D003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call D003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                               
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                        Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                    Else
                                        Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                    End If
                                    If isZ3 = True Then
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    Else
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    End If
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call F002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call F002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    Promotion_Area = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                Else
                                    Promotion_Area = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call F003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call F003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                            If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                            End If
                        Else
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, "", "", Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    New_Price = ""
                                    Discount_Percent = ""
                                End If
                                If isZ3 = True Then
                                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                Else
                                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                End If
                                Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                                Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                                Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    If isZ3 = True Then
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "CPS3_", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    Else
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "", Online_EN, Online_TH, "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Promotion_Area)
                                    End If
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                                Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                                Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                                If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                                    Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                            If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                            End If
                        End If
                        If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                            M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                        End If
                    Next M_Number
                End If
            End If
            
            If Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P011" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P001" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P015" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P103" Then
                Call Fill_Card_Type
            End If
            
            '-------------------FILL CONTRACT------------------------
            If is_Compensate = True Then
                Call Fill_Contract
            End If
            
            BBY_Sheet.Activate
                
            activebookname = ActiveWorkbook.Name
            Application.Run "Convert_C_to_AB_Template_V2.xlsb!Save_File_MER_C", Save_File_Name
            Call Add_Data_to_SQL(tmp_path_C2AB_Template, Save_File_Name)
            Call Copy_File_To_Approve_Folder(Save_File_Name)
            'Call Workbooks(activebookname).Application.Run("!Save_File_MER_C,", DC_File_Name)
            '-------------------------------------------
                    
            '----------Clear From prepare new File----------------
            'Workbooks(activebookname).Application.Run ("!Action_Confirm_clearcells,")
            Set Save_book = Workbooks.Open(Save_File_Name)
            Application.DisplayAlerts = False
            Save_book.Close
            Application.DisplayAlerts = True
            If is_Online_Material = True And InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") = 0 Then '
                fso.CopyFile path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template, tmp_path_C2AB_Template, True
                Call Generate_Online_File(Promo_start_date, Promo_end_date, Theme, Promotion_Name, Online_EN, Online_TH, WBS, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, All_day, Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun, Contract_Type, row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem, Rebate_Reason)
            End If
        End If
    End If
End Sub

Private Function GetPlant(Row_Number As Integer) As String()
    Dim All_Plant, Online_Plant As String
    Dim Plant_List() As String
    Dim Array_Size, Plant_Index As Integer
    Dim Online_Offline As Boolean
    Online_Offline = is_Offline_And_Online(Row_Number, Row_Number, ActiveSheet)
    
    All_Plant = ActiveSheet.Cells(Row_Number, 2).Value2
    Online_Plant = ActiveSheet.Cells(Row_Number, CInt(col_no_plant_end)).Value2
    
    If All_Plant = "X" Or All_Plant = "x" Then
        Array_Size = 1
        ReDim Preserve Plant_List(Array_Size - 1)
        Plant_List(0) = "KA_EXCEPT_ONLINE"
    ElseIf (Online_Plant = "X" Or Online_Plant = "x") And Online_Offline = False Then
        ReDim Preserve Plant_List(0)
        Plant_List(0) = "22KA"
    Else
        Array_Size = 0
        Plant_Index = 0
        Dim p As Integer
        For p = CInt(col_no_plant_start) To CInt(col_no_plant_end) - 1
            If ActiveSheet.Cells(Row_Number, p).Value2 = "X" Or ActiveSheet.Cells(Row_Number, p).Value2 = "x" Then
                Array_Size = Array_Size + 1
            End If
        Next p
        ReDim Preserve Plant_List(Array_Size - 1)
        For p = CInt(col_no_plant_start) To CInt(col_no_plant_end) - 1
            If ActiveSheet.Cells(Row_Number, p).Value2 = "X" Or ActiveSheet.Cells(Row_Number, p).Value2 = "x" Then
                Plant_List(Plant_Index) = Replace(ActiveSheet.Cells(CInt(row_plant_no), p).Value2, Chr(10), "")
                Plant_Index = Plant_Index + 1
            End If
        Next p
    End If
    GetPlant = Plant_List
End Function

Private Function BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic As String) As String()
    Dim Mechanic_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set Mechanic_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mechanic)
    Dim BuyAndGet() As String
    ReDim Preserve BuyAndGet(1)
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    For curr_row_no = 3 To 53
        If Mechanic = Mechanic_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, 1).Value2 Then
            BuyAndGet(0) = Mechanic_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, 5).Value2 'Buy QTY
            BuyAndGet(1) = Mechanic_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, 6).Value2 'Get QTY
            Exit For
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    BuyAndGetQty = BuyAndGet
End Function

Private Function GetQtyPerSet(Mechanic As String) As String
    Dim Mechanic_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set Mechanic_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mechanic)
    Dim QtyPerSet As String
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    For curr_row_no = 3 To 53
        If Mechanic = Mechanic_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, 1).Value2 Then
            QtyPerSet = Mechanic_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, 7).Value2 'QTY Per Set
            Exit For
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    GetQtyPerSet = QtyPerSet
End Function

Function GetUniqueValuesFromColumnIntoArray(WS As Worksheet, ColumnName As String) As String()
     Dim WS_ColumnName_LastRow As Long
     Dim curr_row_no As Long
     Dim j As Long
     Dim Counter As Long
     Dim AllValues() As String
     Dim UniqueValues() As String
     Dim ValueFound As Boolean

     With WS
          WS_ColumnName_LastRow = .Cells(.Rows.Count, ColumnName).End(xlUp).Row
     End With

     ReDim AllValues(0 To WS_ColumnName_LastRow - 4)
    'WS_ColumnName_LastRow = WS_ColumnName_LastRow - 4
     For curr_row_no = 4 To WS_ColumnName_LastRow
          AllValues(curr_row_no - 4) = WS.Range(ColumnName & curr_row_no).Value2
     Next curr_row_no

     ReDim UniqueValues(0 To WS_ColumnName_LastRow - 4)
     UniqueValues(0) = AllValues(0)
     Counter = 0
     For curr_row_no = 1 To WS_ColumnName_LastRow - 4
          ValueFound = False
          For j = 1 To Counter
               If StrComp(AllValues(curr_row_no - 1), UniqueValues(j - 1), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                    ValueFound = True
                    Exit For
               End If
          Next j
          If ValueFound = False Then
               Counter = Counter + 1
               UniqueValues(Counter - 1) = AllValues(curr_row_no - 1)
          End If
     Next curr_row_no

     ReDim Preserve UniqueValues(0 To Counter)
     GetUniqueValuesFromColumnIntoArray = UniqueValues

End Function

Private Sub Fill_BBY_Header(Promotion_Name As String, Purchasing_Group As String, Theme As String, Bonus_buy_Profile As String, Contract_Type As String, Vendor_Code As String, Promo_start_date As String, Promo_end_date As String, Mon As String, Tue As String, Wed As String, Thu As String, Fri As String, Sat As String, Sun As String, All_day As String, Rebate_Reason As String) 'Add Rebate Reason 11-07-2024
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    Set Sub_Con_Sheet = Sheets("Sub_condition")
    
    'Set BBY sheet header
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    BBY_Sheet.Range("C8").Value2 = Promotion_Name
    BBY_Sheet.Range("C9").Value2 = Purchasing_Group
    
    If Len(Theme) = 4 Then
        Dim t As Integer
        For t = 3 To 149
            If Theme = Sub_Con_Sheet.Range("J" & t).Value2 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("F9").Value2 = Sub_Con_Sheet.Range("J" & t).Value2
                Exit For
            End If
        Next t
    Else
        BBY_Sheet.Range("F9").Value2 = Theme
    End If
    
    BBY_Sheet.Range("C10").Value2 = Bonus_buy_Profile
   
    If Len(Trim(Contract_Type)) > 0 Then
        BBY_Sheet.Range("C12").Value2 = Rebate_Reason 'Change from Y 11-07-2024
        BBY_Sheet.Range("E12").Value2 = Contract_Type
    Else
         BBY_Sheet.Range("C12").Value2 = Rebate_Reason 'Change from N 11-07-2024
    End If
    BBY_Sheet.Range("P11").Value2 = Promo_start_date
    BBY_Sheet.Range("P12").Value2 = Promo_end_date
    BBY_Sheet.Range("L12").Value2 = Vendor_Code
    
    Dim is_Day_require As Boolean
    Dim Day_require As String
    Day_require = BBY_Sheet.Range("O14").Value2
    If Trim(Day_require) = "N/A" Then
        is_Day_require = False
    Else
        is_Day_require = True
    End If

    If is_Day_require = True Then
        If All_day <> "" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("P17").Value2 = All_day
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("P19").Value2 = Mon
            BBY_Sheet.Range("P20").Value2 = Tue
            BBY_Sheet.Range("P21").Value2 = Wed
            BBY_Sheet.Range("P22").Value2 = Thu
            BBY_Sheet.Range("P23").Value2 = Fri
            BBY_Sheet.Range("P24").Value2 = Sat
            BBY_Sheet.Range("P25").Value2 = Sun
        End If
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub Mark_Plant(ByRef Plant_List() As String)
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    Dim Plant_Select As Variant
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    For Each Plant_Select In Plant_List
        If Plant_Select = "KA_EXCEPT_ONLINE" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E23").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "13KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E25").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "14KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E26").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "15KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E27").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "16KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E28").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "17KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E29").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "33KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E30").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "30KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E31").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "31KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E32").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "32KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E33").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "34KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E34").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "35KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E35").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "60KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E36").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "61KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E37").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "64KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E38").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "65KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E39").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "67KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E40").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "68KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E41").Value2 = "X"
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "22KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E17").Value2 = "X"
        'Add 66KA 24-04-2023
        ElseIf Plant_Select = "66KA" Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("E42").Value2 = "X"
        End If
    Next Plant_Select
End Sub

Private Function Check_DC(std_C_Sheet As Worksheet) As Boolean
    Dim Profile_type As String
    Dim Purchasing_Group As String
    Dim Mechanic As String
    Dim is_DC As Boolean
    Dim Plant As String
    Dim c_lRow As Long
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    c_lRow = Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row
    
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        Profile_type = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_Profile)).Value2
        Purchasing_Group = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_PurGroup)).Value2
        Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
        
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_FlowType)).Value2 = "DC" Then 'Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_FlowType)).value2 = "FDC"
            If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_IM_Reject)).Value2 <> "Reject" Then
                is_DC = True
                If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_FlowType)).Value2 = "DC" Then
                    Plant = "91KA"
                'ElseIf std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_FlowType)).value2 = "FDC" Then
                '    Plant = "92KA"
                End If
                'Purchasing_Group = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_PurGroup)).value2
                Exit For
            Else
                is_DC = False
            End If
        Else
            is_DC = False
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    Check_DC = is_DC
End Function

Private Sub Gen_DC_File(Plant As String, Promo_start_year As String, Start_Month As String, Start_Day As String, Purchasing_Group As String, Profile_type As String, Mat_Group_Number_List() As String, Mat_Group_Name_List() As String, Vendor_Code As String, is_Contract As Boolean)
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    
    Dim DC_File_Name As String
    Dim BuyAndGet() As String
    ReDim Preserve BuyAndGet(1)
    
    Dim Date_Now As Date
    Date_Now = Now()
    Dim Year_Now As Integer
    Year_Now = CInt(Format(Now(), "yyyy"))
    If Year_Now > 2500 Then
        Date_Now = DateAdd("yyyy", -543, Date_Now)
    End If
    
    
    If is_Contract = True Then
        DC_File_Name = path_dir_output_tmp & CStr(Promo_start_year) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Month), 2) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Day), 2) & "_" & Left(Profile_type, 4) & "_C_BBY_" & Left(Trim(Purchasing_Group), 3) & "_" & Vendor_Code & "_DC_" & Format(Date_Now, "yyyymmdd") & "_" & Format(Now(), "hhmmss") & "_" & UCase(Prepared_by) & ".xlsx"
    Else
        DC_File_Name = path_dir_output_tmp & CStr(Promo_start_year) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Month), 2) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Day), 2) & "_" & Left(Profile_type, 4) & "_C_BBY_" & Left(Trim(Purchasing_Group), 3) & "_" & Vendor_Code & "_DC_" & Format(Date_Now, "yyyymmdd") & "_" & Format(Now(), "hhmmss") & "_" & UCase(Prepared_by) & ".xlsx"
    End If
   
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer
    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14
    
    Dim Promo_start_time As String
    Dim Promo_end_time As String
    Dim WBS As String
    Dim c_lRow As Long
    Dim C_lCol As Long
    Dim Online_EN As String
    Dim Online_TH As String
    Dim Get_Item_Index As Integer
    Get_Item_Index = 14
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    c_lRow = Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row 'Find the last non-blank cell in column Z
    C_lCol = Cells(CInt(row_start), Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column 'Find the last non-blank cell in row 16
    WBS = std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_WBS_no).Value2
    Promo_start_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("C9").Value2
    Promo_end_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("F9").Value2
    
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    If Plant = "91KA" Then
        BBY_Sheet.Range("E47").Value2 = "X"
    ElseIf Plant = "92KA" Then
        BBY_Sheet.Range("E48").Value2 = "X"
    End If
    
    Dim Material_Number As String
    Dim New_Price As String
    Dim Discount_Amount As String
    Dim Discount_Percent As String
    Dim Mechanic  As String
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer
    Bonus_Buy_Index = 1
    Dim Mat_Group_Array_Size As Integer
    Dim is_Mat_Group As Boolean
    Dim Current_Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer
    Dim Normal_Price As String
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Name As String
    Dim Mat_Group_Index As Integer
    Dim M_Number As Variant
    Dim M_Number_Index As Integer
    Dim Sale_Unit As String
    M_Number_Index = 0
    Dim is_Reject_Row As Boolean
    
    If Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2) > 0 Then
        is_Mat_Group = True
    Else
        is_Mat_Group = False
    End If

    If is_Mat_Group = False Then
        For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
            is_Reject_Row = Check_Reject_Row(curr_row_no, std_C_Sheet)
            If is_Reject_Row = False Then
                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                
                Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                New_Price = ""
                Discount_Amount = ""
                Discount_Percent = ""
                If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                    Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                Else
                    
'                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                    Else
'                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                    End If
                    '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                        '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                    Else
                        If InStr(Plant, "22KA") > 0 Then
                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                        Else
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                            Else
                                '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If

                End If
                Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_FlowType)).Value2 = "DC" Then
                    Mat_Group_Array_Size = UBound(Mat_Group_Number_List)
                    If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                            Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                              
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)

                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                End If

                            End If
                            If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                            End If
                    Else
                        Mat_Group_Array_Size = UBound(Mat_Group_Number_List)
                        If is_Mat_Group = True And UBound(Mat_Group_Name_List) > 0 Then
                            If Current_Bonus_Buy_Index <> Bonus_Buy_Index Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                        Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                        Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                        Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                        Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                    End If
                                    Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index
                                End If
                            End If
                            ' Fill Data Get Side
                            ' Noted MGPNew - New Material Group,MAT - Material
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, "", "", Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
