                                Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                End If
                            End If
                        Else
                            If Current_Bonus_Buy_Index <> Bonus_Buy_Index Then
                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                    Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                    Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                    Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                    Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                End If
                            End If
                            ' Fill Data Get Side
                            ' Noted MGPNew - New Material Group,MAT - Material
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                                Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, "", "", Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                                Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                                Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, curr_row_no - CInt(row_start), Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                            ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                                    If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                                        Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                        Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                    Else
                                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                                        Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                                        Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                        If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                            Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next curr_row_no
    Else
        For Each M_Number In Mat_Group_Number_List
            For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                If M_Number = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2) Then
                    Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                    
                    Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                    Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                    New_Price = ""
                    Discount_Amount = ""
                    Discount_Percent = ""
                    If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                        New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                        Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                    Else

'                        If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
'                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                        Else
'                            Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                        End If
                        '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                        If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                            '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                        Else
                            If InStr(Plant, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                            Else
                                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                Else
                                    '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                    Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If

                    End If
                    Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                    Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                    Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                    std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                End If
                
            Next curr_row_no
            If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index
                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                    Call P001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                    Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                'ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                '    Call P010_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                '    Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                'ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                '    Call P011_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                '    Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                    Call P015_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                    Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                    Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                    Call D002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                    Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                    Call D003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                    Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                    If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                        Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                        M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                        Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                    End If
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                    Call F002_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                    Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                    If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                        Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                    End If
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                    Call F003_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                    Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                    If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                        Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                    End If
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                End If
                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                End If
            Else
                If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P001" Then
                    Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, "", "", Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                'ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                '    Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                'ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                '    Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                    Call Gen_P015(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Plant, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                    Call D001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                    Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                    Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                    Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                    Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                    If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                        Call F001_Header(BBY_Sheet, Bonus_Buy_Index, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, WBS, "DC_", "", "", "", Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, "P1")
                        Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                        M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                        Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                    End If
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                    Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                    If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                        Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                    End If
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                    Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, True, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                    If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                        Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                    End If
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                End If
                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                End If
            End If
        Next M_Number
    End If
    If Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P011" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P001" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P015" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P103" Then
        Call Fill_Card_Type
    End If
    '-------------------FILL CONTRACT------------------------
    If is_Contract = True Then
        'Call Fill_Contract
    End If
    
     '----------SAVE DC File---------------------
     'std_Book.Activate
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    Dim activebookname As String
    activebookname = ActiveWorkbook.Name
    'std_Book(BBY_Sheet).Application.Run ("!Save_File_MER_C(" & DC_File_Name & ")")
    'Application.Run "'C:\Users\PIsaraviriyakul\OneDrive - ABeam Consulting Ltd\Desktop\TMG\Sample Template\Convert_C_to_AB_Template.xlsb'!AB10_HideShowColumn.Save_File_MER_C", DC_File_Name
    Application.Run "Convert_C_to_AB_Template_V2.xlsb!Save_File_MER_C", DC_File_Name
    Call Add_Data_to_SQL(tmp_path_C2AB_Template, DC_File_Name)
    Call Copy_File_To_Approve_Folder(DC_File_Name)
    'Call Workbooks(activebookname).Application.Run("!Save_File_MER_C,", DC_File_Name)
    '-------------------------------------------
    Dim Save_book As Workbook
    Set Save_book = Workbooks.Open(DC_File_Name)
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    Save_book.Close
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    
    Set Save_book = Workbooks.Open(DC_File_Name)
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    Save_book.Close
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    'Gen Normal file

End Sub

Private Sub Gen_P001(Mechanic As String, Mat_Group_Index As Integer, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Material_Number As String, New_Price As String, Discount_Amount As String, Discount_Percent As String, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, is_Mat_Group As Boolean, is_DC As Boolean, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, Plant_Str As String, Mat_Group_Name_List() As String, Normal_Price As String, Sale_Unit As String)
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    
    Dim BuyAndGet() As String
    
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    If is_DC = True Then
        'Get Side
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later
        
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    Else
        'Get Side
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later

        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub P001_Header(BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, Promo_start_time As String, Promo_end_time As String, WBS As String, Bonus_Buy_Name_Prefix As String, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Promotion_Area As String)
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Name As String
    BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
    BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
    BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promotion_Area ' Promotion Area ******** DC Fixed P1
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
    Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
    Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
    BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name_Prefix & Bonus_Buy_Name
End Sub

Private Sub Gen_P010(Mechanic As String, Mat_Group_Index As Integer, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Material_Number As String, New_Price As String, Discount_Amount As String, Discount_Percent As String, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, is_Mat_Group As Boolean, is_DC As Boolean, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, Plant_Str As String, is_Promotag As Boolean, Normal_Price As String, Mat_Group_Name_List() As String, Sale_Unit As String)
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    Dim BuyAndGet() As String
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    
    If is_DC = True Then
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        'Material
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        'Discount Type
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    Else
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        'Material
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        'Discount Type
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
        
        'If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
        '    BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).value2 = Online_EN
        '    BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).value2 = Online_TH
        'End If
    End If
    
    'If is_Promotag = True Then
    '    BBY_Sheet.Range("AI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).value2 = "QB"
    'End If
End Sub

    Dim Bonus_Buy_Name As String
    BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promotion_Area ' Promotion Area ******** DC Fixed P1
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Reference_Code
    Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2

Private Sub D002_Header(BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, Promo_start_time As String, Promo_end_time As String, WBS As String, Bonus_Buy_Name_Prefix As String, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, Reference_Code As String, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Promotion_Area As String)
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Name As String
    BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
    BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
    BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promotion_Area ' Promotion Area ******** DC Fixed P1
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
    Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
    BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name_Prefix & Bonus_Buy_Name
    If Promotion_Area = "P4" Then
        BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
        BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub Gen_D003(Mechanic As String, Mat_Group_Index As Integer, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Material_Number As String, New_Price As String, Discount_Amount As String, Discount_Percent As String, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, is_Mat_Group As Boolean, is_DC As Boolean, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, Plant_Str As String, Mat_Group_Number_List() As String, Sale_Unit As String)
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    Dim BuyAndGet() As String
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    
    BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
    
    'Buy Side
    If Mechanic = "A (Coupon)+B (A)" Then
        BBY_Sheet.Range("AN" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("AO" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("AQ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Number_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("AO" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("AQ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        
        BBY_Sheet.Range("AV" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(0)
    End If
    
    'Get Side
    If Mechanic = "A (Coupon)+B (B)" Then
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = Mat_Group_Number_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
                                
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = New_Price
                                
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = Discount_Amount
                                
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
                                
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index - 1).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub D003_Header(BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, Promo_start_time As String, Promo_end_time As String, WBS As String, Bonus_Buy_Name_Prefix As String, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, Reference_Code As String, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Promotion_Area As String)
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Name As String
    BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
    BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time

    Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2

End Sub

Private Sub Gen_P100(Mechanic As String, Mat_Group_Index As Integer, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Material_Number As String, New_Price As String, Discount_Amount As String, Discount_Percent As String, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, is_Mat_Group As Boolean, is_DC As Boolean, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, Plant_Str As String, Mat_Group_Name_List() As String, Normal_Price As String, Sale_Unit As String)
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    
    Dim BuyAndGet() As String
    
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    If is_DC = True Then
        'Get Side
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later
        
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    Else
        'Get Side
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later

        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub Gen_P015(Mechanic As String, Mat_Group_Index As Integer, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Material_Number As String, New_Price As String, Discount_Amount As String, Discount_Percent As String, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, is_Mat_Group As Boolean, is_DC As Boolean, Plant_Str As String, Mat_Group_Name_List() As String, Normal_Price As String, Sale_Unit As String)
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, std_Sheet As Worksheet, Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet, Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    
    Dim BuyAndGet() As String
    
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    If is_DC = True Then
        'Get Side
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later
        
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    Else
        'Get Side
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later

        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub P103_Header(BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, Promo_start_time As String, Promo_end_time As String, WBS As String, Bonus_Buy_Name_Prefix As String, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, Reference_Code As String, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Promotion_Area As String)
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Name As String
    BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promotion_Area ' Promotion Area ******** DC Fixed P1
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
    BBY_Sheet.Range("AI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Reference_Code
    Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
    BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name_Prefix & Bonus_Buy_Name
    If Promotion_Area = "P4" Then
        BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
        BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
    End If
End Sub

 
Private Sub Gen_P103(Mechanic As String, Mat_Group_Index As Integer, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer, Material_Number As String, New_Price As String, Discount_Amount As String, Discount_Percent As String, Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer, is_Mat_Group As Boolean, is_DC As Boolean, Plant_Str As String, Mat_Group_Name_List() As String, Normal_Price As String, Sale_Unit As String)
    'Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim BBY_Sheet As Worksheet
    'Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    'Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    
    Dim BuyAndGet() As String
    
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    If is_DC = True Then
        'Get Side
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later
        
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    Else
        'Get Side
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BF" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
        If is_Mat_Group = True Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MGPNew - New Material Group"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Mat_Group_Name_List(Mat_Group_Index)
        Else
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BG" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "MAT - Material"
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BI" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Material_Number
        End If
        If Len(Trim(New_Price)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BW" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = New_Price
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Amount)) > 0 Then
            BBY_Sheet.Range("BZ" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Amount
        ElseIf Len(Trim(Discount_Percent)) > 0 Then
            If Discount_Percent / 100 < 1 Then
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            Else
                BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Discount_Percent / 100
                If BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 > 100 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 / 100
                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 < 1 Then
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("CA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 * 100
                End If
            End If
        End If
        BuyAndGet = BuyAndGetQty(Mechanic) 'index 0 --> BUY, Index 1 --> GET
        BBY_Sheet.Range("BP" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = BuyAndGet(1)
        BBY_Sheet.Range("CE" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Sale_Unit ' Change Later

        BBY_Sheet.Range("BM" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Normal_Price
    End If
End Sub

Private Function Get_Sale_Org(Plant As String) As String
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim Sub_Con As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set Sub_Con = Sheets("Sub_condition")
    
    Dim SaleOrg As String
    
    Dim s As Integer
    Sub_Con.Activate
    For s = 2 To 29
        If Plant = Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_col_letter_Plant_Sale_Org & CStr(s)).Value2 Then
            SaleOrg = Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_SubCon_col_letter_SaleOrg & CStr(s)).Value2
            Exit For
        End If
    Next s
    
    Get_Sale_Org = SaleOrg
    
End Function

Private Sub Fill_Contract()
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim BBY_Sheet As Worksheet
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet
    Dim Sub_Con As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set Sub_Con = Sheets("Sub_condition")
    
    Dim Condition_table As String, Contract_Type As String, Settlement_Option As String, Settlement_Plant As String, Mechanic As String
    Dim Condition_Rate As String
    Dim Condition_Rate_Percent As String
    Dim Promo_start_date As String
    Dim Promo_end_date As String
    Dim Plant_List() As String
    Dim Plant_Str As String
    Dim Field_Combination As String
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Number As String
    Dim Bonus_Buy_List() As String
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Number_Str As String
    Dim Index_BBY As Integer
    
    Dim Billing_Type_List() As String
    Dim Billing_Type As Variant
    
    Dim BBY_Name As String
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    BBY_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U14").Value2
    
    Dim c_lRow As Long
    Dim C_lCol As Long
    
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    
    Condition_table = std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_ConditionTable)).Value2
    Condition_table = Left(Condition_table, 5)
    Promo_start_date = Right("0" & std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value2, 2) & "." & Right("0" & std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value2, 2) & "." & std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value2
    Promo_end_date = Right("0" & std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_day).Value2, 2) & "." & Right("0" & std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_month).Value2, 2) & "." & std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_year).Value2
    
    'Field_Combination = "Z223"
    
    Dim Start_Date_DT As Date
    Dim End_Date_DT As Date
    Dim End_Of_Month_DT As Date
    
    Start_Date_DT = DateSerial(CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value2), CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value2), CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value2))
    End_Date_DT = DateSerial(CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_year).Value2), CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_month).Value2), CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_day).Value2))
    End_Of_Month_DT = DateSerial(CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value2), CInt(std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value2), 1)
    End_Of_Month_DT = DateAdd("m", 1, End_Of_Month_DT)
    End_Of_Month_DT = DateAdd("d", -1, End_Of_Month_DT)
    
    Dim Promotion_Name As String
    Dim Sale_Org_List() As String
    Dim Sale_Org As String
    Dim Sale_Org_Str As String
    Dim Plant As Variant
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    c_lRow = Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row 'Find the last non-blank cell in column Z
    C_lCol = Cells(CInt(row_start), Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column 'Find the last non-blank cell in row 16
    
    Dim is_DC As Boolean
    Dim is_FDC As Boolean
    Dim is_KA As Boolean
    Dim is_XD As Boolean
    
    BBY_Sheet.Activate
    
    'Add 20-Sep-2024
    Contract_Type = BBY_Sheet.Range("E12").Value2
    Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, col_no_Mechanic).Value2
    'End Add
    
    If BBY_Sheet.Range("E47").Value2 = "X" Then
        is_FDC = True
    End If
    
    If BBY_Sheet.Range("E17").Value2 = "X" Then
        is_KA = True
    End If
    
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_FlowType)).Value2 = "XD" Then
            is_XD = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_FlowType)).Value2 = "DC" Then
            is_DC = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    
    'Assign Plant
    If is_KA = False Then
        Plant_List = GetPlant(CInt(row_start))
    Else
        ReDim Preserve Plant_List(0)
        Plant_List(0) = "22KA"
    End If
    
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        If is_DC = True Then
            std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementOption)).Value2 = "1 - Settlement to Settlement Plant"
            std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementPlant)).Value2 = "91KA"
        End If
        
        If is_XD = True Then
            std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementOption)).Value2 = "1 - Settlement to Settlement Plant"
            std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementPlant)).Value2 = "91KA"
        End If
        
        If is_FDC = True Then
            std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementOption)).Value2 = "1 - Settlement to Settlement Plant"
            std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementPlant)).Value2 = "92KA"
        End If
        If is_KA = True Then
            std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementOption)).Value2 = "2 - Settlement to same business volume plant with in company"
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    
    Dim SO_Index As Integer
    SO_Index = 0
    ReDim Preserve Sale_Org_List(SO_Index)
    
    If is_DC = False Then
        Plant_Str = Join(Plant_List, ",")
    End If
 
    Index_BBY = 0
    ReDim Preserve Bonus_Buy_List(Index_BBY)
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        Bonus_Buy_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2
        Bonus_Buy_Number_Str = Join(Bonus_Buy_List, ",")
        If InStr(Bonus_Buy_Number_Str, Bonus_Buy_Number) > 0 Then
        Else
            If Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) > 0 Then
                Bonus_Buy_List(Index_BBY) = Bonus_Buy_Number
                Index_BBY = Index_BBY + 1
                ReDim Preserve Bonus_Buy_List(Index_BBY)
            End If
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    
    If Index_BBY > 0 Then
        ReDim Preserve Bonus_Buy_List(Index_BBY - 1)
    End If
    
    Dim is_Settlement_Plant As Boolean
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    
    ReDim Preserve Billing_Type_List(1)
    Billing_Type_List(0) = "ZFP0"
    Billing_Type_List(1) = "ZFP2"
    

    Promotion_Name = std_C_Sheet.Range("M4").Value2
    If Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementOption)).Value2) > 0 And Left(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementOption)).Value2, 1) <> "2" Then
        If is_KA = False Then
            is_Settlement_Plant = True
            Settlement_Option = std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementOption)).Value2
        Else
            is_Settlement_Plant = False
            Settlement_Option = "2 - Settlement to same business volume plant with in company"
        End If
    Else
        is_Settlement_Plant = False
        Settlement_Option = "2 - Settlement to same business volume plant with in company"
    End If
    
    Dim Plant_Select_List() As String
    Dim Plant_Select_Index As Integer
    Dim Plant_Select_Var As Variant
    Dim Plant_Select_STR As String
    Dim Plant_Select As String
    ReDim Preserve Plant_Select_List(0)
    
    Plant_Select_Index = 0
    
    If is_Settlement_Plant = True Then
        std_C_Sheet.Activate
        For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
            Plant_Select = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementPlant)).Value2
            Plant_Select_STR = Join(Plant_Select_List, ",")
            If InStr(Plant_Select_STR, Plant_Select) > 0 Then
            Else
                Plant_Select_List(Plant_Select_Index) = Plant_Select
                Plant_Select_Index = Plant_Select_Index + 1
                ReDim Preserve Plant_Select_List(Plant_Select_Index)
            End If
        Next curr_row_no
        ReDim Preserve Plant_Select_List(Plant_Select_Index - 1)

    Else
        If InStr(Plant_Str, "KA_EXCEPT_ONLINE") > 0 Then
            ReDim Preserve Sale_Org_List(6)
            Dim s As Integer
            Sub_Con.Activate
            For s = CInt(C2AB_SubCon_row_start_SaleOrg) To CInt(C2AB_SubCon_row_end_SaleOrg)
                Sale_Org_List(SO_Index) = Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_SubCon_col_letter_SaleOrg & CStr(s)).Value2
                SO_Index = SO_Index + 1
            Next s
        Else
            For Each Plant In Plant_List
                Sale_Org = Get_Sale_Org(CStr(Plant))
                Sale_Org_Str = Join(Sale_Org_List, ",")
                If InStr(Sale_Org_Str, Sale_Org) > 0 Then
                Else
                    Sale_Org_List(SO_Index) = Sale_Org
                    SO_Index = SO_Index + 1
                    ReDim Preserve Sale_Org_List(SO_Index)
                End If
            Next Plant
            ReDim Preserve Sale_Org_List(SO_Index - 1)
        End If
    
    End If
    
    Dim Row_index As Integer
    Dim Contract_Number As Integer
    Row_index = 14
    Contract_Number = 1
    
    Dim SO As Variant
    Dim BBY As Variant
    Dim Material_Number As String
    Dim End_Month_Check As Date
    Dim Material_Index As Integer
    Material_Index = Row_index
    Dim QTY_Per_Contract As String
    Dim Payment_Method As String
    Dim Payment_Term As String
    Dim Sale_Unit As String
    
    If is_Settlement_Plant = True Then
        '1 - Settlement to Settlement Plant
        '2 - Settlement to same business volume plant with in company
        For Each BBY In Bonus_Buy_List
            For Each Plant_Select_Var In Plant_Select_List
                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                Settlement_Plant = Plant_Select_Var
                Sale_Org = Get_Sale_Org(Settlement_Plant)
                For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                    If Settlement_Plant = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_SettlementPlant)).Value2 And BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                        Bonus_Buy_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2
                        Payment_Method = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_PaymentMethod)).Value2
                        Payment_Term = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_PaymentTerms)).Value2
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next curr_row_no
                BBY_Sheet.Activate
                'Condition Header
                BBY_Sheet.Range("ES" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Contract_Number
                'BBY_Sheet.Range("EY" & CStr(Row_index)).value2 = "M - Account Clearing"
                BBY_Sheet.Range("EX" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Payment_Term
                BBY_Sheet.Range("EY" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Payment_Method
                
                BBY_Sheet.Range("EZ" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Sale_Org
                BBY_Sheet.Range("FE" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Settlement_Option
                BBY_Sheet.Range("FF" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Settlement_Plant
                BBY_Sheet.Range("FK" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Promotion_Name
                BBY_Sheet.Range("FH" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "X"
                
                'Check Z222
                If Left(Contract_Type, 4) = "Z222" Then
                    QTY_Per_Contract = GetQtyPerSet(Mechanic)
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("FG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Number
                    BBY_Sheet.Range("FN" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = QTY_Per_Contract
                End If
                
                'Condition Type
                If Condition_table = "V 163" Then
                    Dim m As Integer
                    std_C_Sheet.Activate
                    For m = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                        If is_KA = True Then
                            If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_plant_end)).Value2)) > 0 And Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_OnlineStatus)).Value2)) > 0 Then
                                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                                
                                If Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerQty_SAP)).Value2) > 0 And Left(Contract_Type, 4) <> "Z222" Then
                                    Condition_Rate = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerQty_SAP)).Value2
                                ElseIf Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerSet)).Value2) > 0 Then
                                    Condition_Rate = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerSet)).Value2
                                End If
                                
                                Condition_Rate_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_Percent)).Value2
                                
                                BBY_Sheet.Activate
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("HC" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Contract_Number
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("HD" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Condition_table
                                'If InStr(Condition_Rate, "%") > 0 Then
                                If Len(Trim(Condition_Rate_Percent)) > 0 Then
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HE" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = "ZR01 - Charge Back %"
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Condition_Rate_Percent
                                    If BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 <= 1 Then
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 * 100
                                    ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 > 100 Then
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 / 100
                                    End If
                                Else
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HE" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = "ZR05 - Charge Amount(PeQty)"
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Condition_Rate
                                End If
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("HF" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Material_Number
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("HP" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                                Material_Index = Material_Index + 1
                            End If
                        Else
                            If BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                                
                                If Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerQty_SAP)).Value2) > 0 And Left(Contract_Type, 4) <> "Z222" And BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                                    Condition_Rate = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerQty_SAP)).Value2
                                ElseIf Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerSet)).Value2) > 0 And BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                                    Condition_Rate = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerSet)).Value2
                                End If
                                Condition_Rate_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Compensate_Percent)).Value2
                                If BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                                    BBY_Sheet.Activate
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HC" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Contract_Number
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HD" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Condition_table
                                    'If InStr(Condition_Rate, "%") > 0 Then
                                    If Len(Trim(Condition_Rate_Percent)) > 0 Then
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("HE" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = "ZR01 - Charge Back %"
                                        'BBY_Sheet.Range("HI" & CStr(Material_Index)).value2 = Replace(Condition_Rate, "%", "")
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Condition_Rate_Percent
                                        If BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 <= 1 Then
                                            BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 * 100
                                        ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 > 100 Then
                                            BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 / 100
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("HE" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = "ZR05 - Charge Amount(PeQty)"
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Condition_Rate
                                    End If
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HF" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = Material_Number
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HP" & CStr(Material_Index)).Value2 = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                                    Material_Index = Material_Index + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next m
                Else
                    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow

                            ElseIf Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerSet)).Value2) > 0 Then

                                ElseIf BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 > 100 Then
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 / 100
                                End If
                            Else

                                BBY_Sheet.Range("HM" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Condition_Rate
                            End If
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next curr_row_no
                End If
                
            Next Plant_Select_Var
            Row_index = Row_index + 1
            Contract_Number = Contract_Number + 1
        Next BBY
        Row_index = 14
        Contract_Number = 1
