                                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                                If BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                                    Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                                    Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                                    BBY_Sheet.Activate
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GE" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Contract_Number
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Field_Combination
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = BBY
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GW" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Billing_Type
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GM" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Material_Number
                                    'Add Sale_Unit 23-Sep-2024
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("HA" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Sale_Unit
                                    If Field_Combination = "Z265" Then
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("GP" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZPN1"
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("GK" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Plant_Select_Var
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = ""
                                        'BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "Z266"
                                    ElseIf Field_Combination = "Z256" Then
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("GP" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZPN1"
                                        BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = ""
                                        'BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "Z262"
                                    End If
                                    Row_index = Row_index + 1
                                End If
                            Next m
                        Next Billing_Type
                        'Add 23-Sep-2024 Check Exclusive
                        For m = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                            std_C_Sheet.Activate
                            Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                            Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                            If std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_ContractType_Inc_Exc)).Value2 = "Exclusive" And BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                                BBY_Sheet.Activate
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GE" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Contract_Number
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Field_Combination
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = BBY
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GW" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZFP2"
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GM" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Material_Number
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GI" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_ContractType_Inc_Exc)).Value2
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GZ" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZBL"
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("HA" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Sale_Unit
                                If Field_Combination = "Z265" Then
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GP" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZPN1"
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GK" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Plant_Select_Var
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = ""
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "Z266"
                                ElseIf Field_Combination = "Z256" Then
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GP" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZPN1"
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = ""
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "Z263"
                                End If
                                Row_index = Row_index + 1
                            End If
                        Next m
                    Next Plant_Select_Var
                'Business Volume Sale
                Else
                    For Each Billing_Type In Billing_Type_List
                        For m = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                            std_C_Sheet.Activate
                            If BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                                Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                                Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                                BBY_Sheet.Activate
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GE" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Contract_Number
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Field_Combination
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = BBY
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GW" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Billing_Type
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GM" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Material_Number
                                'Add Sale_Unit 23-Sep-2024
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("HA" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Sale_Unit
                                If Field_Combination = "Z265" Then
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GP" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZPN1"
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = ""
                                ElseIf Field_Combination = "Z256" Then
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GP" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZPN1"
                                    BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = ""
                                End If
                                Row_index = Row_index + 1
                            End If
                        Next m
                    Next Billing_Type
                    'Add 23-Sep-2024 Check Exclusive
                    For m = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                        std_C_Sheet.Activate
                        Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                        Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_ContractType_Inc_Exc)).Value2 = "Exclusive" And BBY = CStr(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Activate
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("GE" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Contract_Number
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Field_Combination
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = BBY
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("GW" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZFP2"
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("GM" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Material_Number
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("GI" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_ChargeBack_ContractType_Inc_Exc)).Value2
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("GZ" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZBL"
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("HA" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Sale_Unit
                            If Field_Combination = "Z265" Then
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GP" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZPN1"
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = ""
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "Z266"
                            ElseIf Field_Combination = "Z256" Then
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GP" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "ZPN1"
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GO" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = ""
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("GG" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = "Z263"
                            End If
                            Row_index = Row_index + 1
                        End If
                    Next m
                'End Add
                End If
                Contract_Number = Contract_Number + 1
            Next SO
        Next BBY
        
        'Contract_number = Contract_number - 1
        End_Month_Check = End_Of_Month_DT
        Row_index = 14
        BBY_Sheet.Activate
        For curr_row_no = 1 To Contract_Number - 1
            BBY_Sheet.Range("HV" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = curr_row_no
            'BBY_Sheet.Range("HS" & CStr(Row_index)).value2 = Right("0" & Day(End_Date_DT), 2) & "." & Right("0" & Month(End_Date_DT), 2) & "." & Year(End_Date_DT)
            BBY_Sheet.Range("HW" & CStr(Row_index)).Value2 = Promo_end_date
            Row_index = Row_index + 1

        Next curr_row_no
        
    End If
    
End Sub

Private Function GetPlant_ForContract(std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, Contract_Number As Integer, BBY_Sheet As Worksheet, Settlement_Option As String) As String()
    Dim Plant_List() As String
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer, Array_Size As Integer, Plant_Index As Integer, p As Integer, Contract_Last_Row As Integer, Sub_Con_Last_Row As Integer
    Dim Sub_Con As Worksheet
    Set Sub_Con = Sheets("Sub_condition")
    Dim Sale_Org As String
    Dim Plant As String
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    If Left(Settlement_Option, 1) = "1" Then
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, 2).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, 2).Value2 = "x" Then
            Array_Size = 0
            Plant_Index = 0
            For p = CInt(col_no_plant_start) To CInt(col_no_plant_end) - 1
                If Left(Replace(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_plant_no), p).Value2, Chr(10), ""), 2) <> "XX" Then
                    Array_Size = Array_Size + 1
                End If
            Next p
            ReDim Preserve Plant_List(Array_Size - 1)
            For curr_row_no = CInt(col_no_plant_start) To CInt(col_no_plant_end) - 1
                If Left(Replace(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_plant_no), curr_row_no).Value2, Chr(10), ""), 2) <> "XX" Then
                    Plant_List(Plant_Index) = Replace(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_plant_no), curr_row_no).Value2, Chr(10), "")
                    Plant_Index = Plant_Index + 1
                End If
            Next curr_row_no
        Else
            For p = CInt(col_no_plant_start) To CInt(col_no_plant_end) - 1
                If std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, p).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, p).Value2 = "x" Then
                    Array_Size = Array_Size + 1
                End If
            Next p
            ReDim Preserve Plant_List(Array_Size - 1)
            For p = CInt(col_no_plant_start) To CInt(col_no_plant_end) - 1
                If std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, p).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, p).Value2 = "x" Then
                    Plant_List(Plant_Index) = Replace(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_plant_no), p).Value2, Chr(10), "")
                    Plant_Index = Plant_Index + 1
                End If
            Next p
        End If
    Else
        BBY_Sheet.Activate
        Contract_Last_Row = Cells(Rows.Count, 149).End(xlUp).Row 'Last Row Column ES
        For curr_row_no = 14 To Contract_Last_Row
            If BBY_Sheet.Range("ES" & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2 = CStr(Contract_Number) Then
                Sale_Org = BBY_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, 156).Value2
                Exit For
            End If
        Next curr_row_no
        Array_Size = 0
        Plant_Index = 0
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, 2).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, 2).Value2 = "x" Then
            Sub_Con.Activate
            For curr_row_no = 2 To 20 'Sub con ORG Row
                If Left(Sale_Org, 4) = Left(Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_SubCon_col_letter_SaleOrg & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2, 4) And Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_col_letter_Plant_Sale_Org & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2 <> "22KA" And Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_col_letter_Plant_Sale_Org & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2 <> "68KA" Then
                    Array_Size = Array_Size + 1
                End If
            Next curr_row_no
            ReDim Preserve Plant_List(Array_Size - 1)
            For curr_row_no = 2 To 20 'Sub con ORG Row
                If Left(Sale_Org, 4) = Left(Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_SubCon_col_letter_SaleOrg & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2, 4) And Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_col_letter_Plant_Sale_Org & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2 <> "22KA" And Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_col_letter_Plant_Sale_Org & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2 <> "68KA" Then
                    Plant_List(Plant_Index) = Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_col_letter_Plant_Sale_Org & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2
                    Plant_Index = Plant_Index + 1
                End If
            Next curr_row_no
        Else ' CHECK WITH CONTRACT NUMBER
            For p = CInt(col_no_plant_start) To CInt(col_no_plant_end) - 1
                std_C_Sheet.Activate
                If std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, p).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, p).Value2 = "x" Then
                    Plant = Replace(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_plant_no), p).Value2, Chr(10), "")
                    For curr_row_no = 2 To 20
                        If Plant = Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_col_letter_Plant_Sale_Org & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2 And Left(Sale_Org, 4) = Left(Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_SubCon_col_letter_SaleOrg & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2, 4) Then
                            Array_Size = Array_Size + 1
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next curr_row_no
                End If
            Next p
            ReDim Preserve Plant_List(Array_Size - 1)
            For p = CInt(col_no_plant_start) To CInt(col_no_plant_end) - 1
                If std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, p).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(row_start, p).Value2 = "x" Then
                    Plant = Replace(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_plant_no), p).Value2, Chr(10), "")
                    For curr_row_no = 2 To 20
                        If Plant = Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_col_letter_Plant_Sale_Org & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2 And Left(Sale_Org, 4) = Left(Sub_Con.Range(C2AB_SubCon_col_letter_SaleOrg & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2, 4) Then
                            Plant_List(Plant_Index) = Replace(std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_plant_no), p).Value2, Chr(10), "")
                            Plant_Index = Plant_Index + 1
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next curr_row_no
                End If
            Next p
        End If
        
    End If
    GetPlant_ForContract = Plant_List
End Function

Private Sub Fill_Card_Type()
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim BBY_Sheet As Worksheet
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet
    Dim Sub_Con As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set Sub_Con = Sheets("Sub_condition")
    Dim c_lRow As Long
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    c_lRow = Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row 'Find the last non-blank cell in column Z
    
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Number As String
    Dim Bonus_Buy_List() As String
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Number_Str As String
    Dim Index_BBY As Integer
    Dim BBY As Variant
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    
    Index_BBY = 0
    ReDim Preserve Bonus_Buy_List(Index_BBY)
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        Bonus_Buy_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2
        Bonus_Buy_Number_Str = Join(Bonus_Buy_List, ",")
        If InStr(Bonus_Buy_Number_Str, Bonus_Buy_Number) > 0 Then
        Else
            If Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2) > 0 Then
                Bonus_Buy_List(Index_BBY) = Bonus_Buy_Number
                Index_BBY = Index_BBY + 1
                ReDim Preserve Bonus_Buy_List(Index_BBY)
            End If
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    If Index_BBY > 0 Then
        ReDim Preserve Bonus_Buy_List(Index_BBY - 1)
    End If
    
    Dim Card_Type As String
    Dim Row_index As Integer
    Dim Card_Type_Row As Integer
    Dim Card_Type_Last_Row As Integer
    Row_index = 14
    Card_Type_Row = 2
    Card_Type_Last_Row = 20
    
    
    For Each BBY In Bonus_Buy_List
        For curr_row_no = Card_Type_Row To Card_Type_Last_Row
            Sub_Con.Activate
            Card_Type = Sub_Con.Range("DZ" & CStr(curr_row_no)).Value2
            BBY_Sheet.Activate
            BBY_Sheet.Range("CR" & Row_index).Value2 = BBY
            BBY_Sheet.Range("CS" & Row_index).Value2 = Card_Type
            Row_index = Row_index + 1
        Next curr_row_no
    Next BBY
End Sub

Private Function Create_Folder() As String
    Dim strFolderName As String
    Dim strFolderExists As String
    strFolderName = CStr(path_dir_tmp)
    
    strFolderExists = Dir(strFolderName, vbDirectory)
    If strFolderExists = "." Then
    Else
        MkDir strFolderName
    End If
    strFolderName = strFolderName & Format(Now(), "yyyymmdd_hhmm") & "\"
    
    strFolderExists = Dir(strFolderName, vbDirectory)
    If strFolderExists = "." Then
    Else
        MkDir strFolderName
    End If
    Create_Folder = strFolderName
End Function

Private Sub Generate_Online_File(Promo_start_date As Date, Promo_end_date As Date, Theme As String, Promotion_Name As String, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, WBS As String, Promo_start_time As String, Promo_end_time As String, All_day As String, Mon As String, Tue As String, Wed As String, Thu As String, Fri As String, Sat As String, Sun As String, Contract_Type As String, row_no_firstPromoItem As Integer, row_no_lastPromoItem As Integer, Rebate_Reason As String)
    fso.CopyFile path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template, tmp_path_C2AB_Template, True

    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim std_C_Sheet As Worksheet
    Dim BBY_Sheet As Worksheet
    Dim Mat_Group_Sheet As Worksheet
    Dim sheet_name_Mer_C_Template As String
    Set sheet_MerC = Worksheets(sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set std_C_Sheet = Worksheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template)
    Set BBY_Sheet = Worksheets(C2AB_sheet_name_BBY)
    Set Mat_Group_Sheet = Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mat_Group)
    Dim Date_Now As Date
    Date_Now = Now()
    Dim Year_Now As Integer
    Year_Now = CInt(Format(Now(), "yyyy"))
    If Year_Now > 2500 Then
        Date_Now = DateAdd("yyyy", -543, Date_Now)
    End If
    
    Call Copy_From_Input_to_STD(wb_MerC, sheet_MerC, std_Book, std_C_Sheet, row_no_firstPromoItem, row_no_lastPromoItem, Promo_start_date, 0, Promotion_Name, Online_EN, Online_TH, WBS, Promo_start_time, Promo_end_time, All_day, Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun, Theme, Promo_end_date)
    
    Dim activebookname As String
    Dim Save_book As Workbook
    Dim Vendor_Code As String
    Dim Start_Day_Fill As String
    Dim End_Day_Fill As String
    Dim Start_Day As String
    Dim Start_Month As String
    Dim Promo_start_year As String
    Dim End_Day As String
    Dim End_Month As String
    Dim Promo_end_year As String
    Dim Purchasing_Group As String
    Dim Profile_type As String
    Dim Mechanic As String
    Dim Plant_List() As String
    ReDim Preserve Plant_List(0)
    Plant_List(0) = "22KA"
    
    Start_Day = Day(Promo_start_date)
    Start_Month = Month(Promo_start_date)
    Promo_start_year = IIf(Year(Promo_start_date) < 2000, Year(Promo_start_date) + 543, Year(Promo_start_date))
    End_Day = Day(Promo_end_date)
    End_Month = Month(Promo_end_date)
    Promo_end_year = IIf(Year(Promo_end_date) < 2000, Year(Promo_end_date) + 543, Year(Promo_end_date))
    
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    Vendor_Code = std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_Vendor)).Value2
    If Len(Trim(Vendor_Code)) = 0 Then
        Vendor_Code = "NOBP"
    End If
    Start_Day_Fill = Right("0" & Start_Day, 2) & "." & Right("0" & Start_Month, 2) & "." & Promo_start_year
    End_Day_Fill = Right("0" & End_Day, 2) & "." & Right("0" & End_Month, 2) & "." & Promo_end_year
    Purchasing_Group = std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_PurGroup)).Value2
    Purchasing_Group = Get_Purchasing_Group_Full_Name(Purchasing_Group)
    Profile_type = std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_BBY_Profile)).Value2
    Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
    
    If Profile_type = "P001" Or Profile_type = "P011" Then
        Application.DisplayAlerts = False
        std_Book.Close
        Application.DisplayAlerts = True
        fso.CopyFile path_sharedFile_C2AB_Template, tmp_path_C2AB_Template, True
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    Dim c_lRow As Long
    c_lRow = Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row 'Find the last non-blank cell in column Material Number
    
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    Dim Mat_Group_Number As String
    Dim Material_Number As String
    Dim Mat_Group_Index As Integer
    Dim MCH_LV2 As String
    Dim Mat_Group_Number_List() As String
    Dim Mat_Group_Name_List() As String
    ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Number_List(0)
    ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Name_List(0)
    Dim is_Mat_Group As Boolean
    Dim is_Compensate As Boolean, isZ3 As Boolean
    Dim is_Reject_Row As Boolean
    
     'Check Mat Group Need
    Dim m As Integer
    For m = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2)) > 0 Then
            is_Mat_Group = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next m
    
    'Check Compensate
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
        If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Compensate_BahtPerQty_SAP)).Value2)) > 0 Or Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Compensate_Percent)).Value2)) > 0 Then
            If Left(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ContractType)).Value2, 2) = "Z2" Then
                is_Compensate = True
                Exit For
            ElseIf Left(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_ContractType)).Value2, 2) = "Z3" Then
                isZ3 = True
                Exit For
            End If
        Else
            is_Compensate = False
            isZ3 = False
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    
    
    
    Mat_Group_Index = 4
    If is_Mat_Group = True Then
        For m = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
            std_C_Sheet.Activate
            If std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2 <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2 <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" And Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_OnlineStatus)).Value2)) > 0 And Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_plant_end)).Value2)) > 0 Then
                is_Reject_Row = Check_Reject_Row(m, std_C_Sheet)
                If is_Reject_Row = False Then
                    If Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_OnlineStatus)).Value2) > 0 And Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_plant_end)).Value2)) > 0 Then
                        Mat_Group_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_Group)).Value2
                        Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                        MCH_LV2 = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_MCH_LV2)).Value2
                       ' Mat_Group_Desc = Promotion_Name & "_" & Mat_Group_Number
                        Mat_Group_Sheet.Activate
                        Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(Mat_Group_Index, 1).Value2 = Mat_Group_Number
                        Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(Mat_Group_Index, 5).Value2 = Material_Number
                        Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(Mat_Group_Index, 6).Value2 = std_C_Sheet.Cells(m, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                        Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(Mat_Group_Index, 4).Value2 = "1 - Material No."
                        Mat_Group_Index = Mat_Group_Index + 1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next m
        Dim M_lRow As Integer
        Dim M_group_Number As String
        Dim M_group_Name As String
        Dim M_Group_Number_STR As String
        Dim M_Group_Name_STR As String
        Dim M_Index As Integer
        Dim M_Number_Index As Integer
        Dim Get_Item_Index As Integer
        
        M_Index = 0
        M_Number_Index = 0
        Mat_Group_Sheet.Activate
        M_lRow = Cells(Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
        For m = 4 To M_lRow
            M_group_Name = Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(m, 2).Value2
            M_group_Number = Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(m, 1).Value2
            M_Group_Name_STR = Join(Mat_Group_Name_List, ",")
            M_Group_Number_STR = Join(Mat_Group_Number_List, ",")
            
            If InStr(M_Group_Name_STR, M_group_Name) > 0 Then
            Else
                If Len(Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(m, 2).Value2) > 0 Then
                    Mat_Group_Name_List(M_Index) = M_group_Name
                    M_Index = M_Index + 1
                    ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Name_List(M_Index)
                End If
            End If
            If InStr(M_Group_Number_STR, M_group_Number) > 0 Then
            Else
                If Len(Mat_Group_Sheet.Cells(m, 1).Value2) > 0 Then
                    Mat_Group_Number_List(M_Number_Index) = M_group_Number
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                    ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Number_List(M_Number_Index)
                End If
            End If
        Next m
        ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Name_List(M_Index - 1)
        ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Number_List(M_Number_Index - 1)
    Else
        ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Name_List(0)
        ReDim Preserve Mat_Group_Number_List(0)
    End If
    
    Profile_type = Get_Profile_Type_Full_Name(Profile_type)
    
    Call Fill_BBY_Header(Promotion_Name, Purchasing_Group, Theme, Profile_type, Contract_Type, Vendor_Code, Start_Day_Fill, End_Day_Fill, Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun, All_day, Rebate_Reason)
    
    Dim KA_File_Name As String
    KA_File_Name = path_dir_output_tmp & CStr(Promo_start_year) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Month), 2) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Day), 2) & "_" & Left(Profile_type, 4) & "_C_BBY_" & Left(Trim(Purchasing_Group), 3) & "_" & Vendor_Code & "_KA_" & Format(Date_Now, "yyyymmdd") & "_" & Format(Now(), "hhmmss") & "_" & UCase(Prepared_by) & ".xlsx"
    
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Row_Index As Integer
    Dim Current_Bonus_Buy_Index As Integer
    Dim BBY_Check As Integer
    'Dim M_Number_Index As Integer
    Dim Plant_Str As String
    
    Bonus_Buy_Index = 1
    Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14
    Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = 1
    BBY_Check = 0
    M_Number_Index = 0
    
    Dim New_Price As String
    Dim Discount_Amount As String
    Dim Discount_Percent As String
    Dim Normal_Price As String
    Dim Sale_Unit As String
    Dim Current_Bonus_Buy_Number As Integer
    Dim Bonus_Buy_Name As String
    Dim M_Number As Variant
    
    If is_Compensate = True Then
        KA_File_Name = path_dir_output_tmp & CStr(Promo_start_year) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Month), 2) & Right("0" & CStr(Start_Day), 2) & "_" & Left(Profile_type, 4) & "_C_BBYCT_" & Left(Trim(Purchasing_Group), 3) & "_" & Vendor_Code & "_KA_" & Format(Date_Now, "yyyymmdd") & "_" & Format(Now(), "hhmmss") & "_" & UCase(Prepared_by) & ".xlsx"
        Plant_Str = Join(Plant_List, ",")
        Call Mark_Plant(Plant_List)
        If is_Mat_Group = False Then
            For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To c_lRow
                is_Reject_Row = Check_Reject_Row(curr_row_no, std_C_Sheet)
                If is_Reject_Row = False And Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_OnlineStatus)).Value2)) > 0 And Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_plant_end)).Value2)) > 0 Then
                    If Len(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_OnlineStatus)).Value2) > 0 Then
                        Material_Number = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mat_no)).Value2
                       
                        Online_EN = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_EN)).Value2
                        Online_TH = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Online_TH)).Value2
                        New_Price = ""
                        Discount_Amount = ""
                        Discount_Percent = ""
                        If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                            Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                            Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                        Else

                            'If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then  ----2023/09/18 by Pakornpat.sup
'                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Or Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Or Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then '----2023/09/18 by Pakornpat.sup
'                                New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                            Else
'                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                            End If
                            '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Or Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                            Else
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                Else
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                    Else

                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If

                        Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                        Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                        If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                            std_C_Sheet.Activate
                            WBS = std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_WBS_no).Value2
                            Promo_start_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("C9").Value2
                            Promo_end_time = std_C_Sheet.Range("F9").Value2

                            If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then

                                BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time

        Else

                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1

                        Discount_Percent = ""
                        If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                            New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                            Discount_Amount = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Discount_Amt)).Value2
                            Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                        Else
'                            'If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then  ----2023/09/18 by Pakornpat.sup
'                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Or Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Or Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then '----2023/09/18 by Pakornpat.sup
'                                New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
'                            Else
'                                Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
'                            End If
                            '---Barcode <= 5 digit---
                            If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Or Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then '---Barcode <= 5 digit and Profile D001---
                                '---New_Price use Discount_Type_New_Price---
                                New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                            Else
                                If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                    New_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesPrice_Promo)).Value2
                                Else
                                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P015" Then
                                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_P015)).Value2
                                    Else
                                        '---Discount_Percent use Discount_Percent_IM
                                        Discount_Percent = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_DiscountPercent_PLU)).Value2
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If

                        End If
                        Mechanic = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Mechanic)).Value2
                        Normal_Price = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalePrice_Normal)).Value2
                        Sale_Unit = std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_SalesUnit)).Value2
                        std_C_Sheet.Activate
                        std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_BBY_no)).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index
                    End If
                    
                Next curr_row_no
                BBY_Sheet.Activate
                If Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = 14 Then
                    Current_Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index
                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P100" Then
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
                        Else
                             BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                        End If

                        BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
                        Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name
                        Call Gen_P001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P103" Then
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
                        Else
                             BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                        End If

                        If isZ3 = True Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Replace(Bonus_Buy_Name, "KA_", "KA_CPS3_")
                        Else
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name
                        End If
                        Call Gen_P103(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                    
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
                        Else

                        End If
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
                        Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
                        If isZ3 = True Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Replace(Bonus_Buy_Name, "KA_", "KA_CPS3_")
                        Else
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name
                        End If
                        'If Len(Trim(std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_Barcode)).Value2)) > 5 Then
                            New_Price = ""
                            Discount_Percent = ""
                        'End If
                        Call Gen_D001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                        Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
                        Else
                             BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                        End If
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
                        Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
                        If isZ3 = True Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Replace(Bonus_Buy_Name, "KA_", "KA_CPS3_")
                        Else
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name
                        End If
                        Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                        
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time

                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
                        Else
                             BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                        End If
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
                        Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
                        If isZ3 = True Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Replace(Bonus_Buy_Name, "KA_", "KA_CPS3_")
                        Else
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name
                        End If

                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                        If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
                            If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
                            Else
                                 BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                            End If
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
                            Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
                            If isZ3 = True Then
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Replace(Bonus_Buy_Name, "KA_", "KA_CPS3_")

                            End If
                            Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                        End If
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F002" Then
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
                        Else
                             BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                        End If
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS
                        Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2
                        If isZ3 = True Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Replace(Bonus_Buy_Name, "KA_", "KA_CPS3_")
                        Else
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name
                        End If
                        Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                        If Mechanic = "Get Group (1 Group)" Then
                            Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                        End If
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F003" Then
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time
                        If InStr(Plant_Str, "22KA") > 0 Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH
                        Else
                             BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P1" ' Promotion Area
                        End If
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS

                        If isZ3 = True Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Replace(Bonus_Buy_Name, "KA_", "KA_CPS3_")
                        Else
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Name
                        End If
                        Call Gen_F003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)
                        If Mechanic = "1A Get 1B (B)" Or Mechanic = "1A Get 2B (B)" Or Mechanic = "2A Get 1B (B)" Then
                            Get_Item_Index = Get_Item_Index + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                    If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                        Bonus_Buy_Row_Index = Bonus_Buy_Row_Index + 1
                    End If
                Else
                    If Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P100" Then
                        Call Gen_P100(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, "", "", Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P010" Then
                        Call Gen_P010(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, False, Normal_Price, Mat_Group_Name_List, Sale_Unit)
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P011" Then
                        Call Gen_P011(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "P103" Then
                        Call Gen_P103(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Name_List, Normal_Price, Sale_Unit)
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D001" Then
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                        BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time

                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH

                        End If

                        Bonus_Buy_Name = BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2

                            New_Price = ""

                        Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D002" Then
                        Call Gen_D002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "D003" Then
                        Call Gen_D003(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                    ElseIf Left(Profile_type, 4) = "F001" Then
                        If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("T" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Bonus_Buy_Index ' Bonus Buy Number
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("W" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_start_time ' Start time
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("X" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Promo_end_time 'End time

                                BBY_Sheet.Range("AA" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = "P4" ' Promotion Area
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("AK" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_EN
                                BBY_Sheet.Range("AL" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Online_TH

                            End If
                            BBY_Sheet.Range("AB" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = WBS

                                BBY_Sheet.Range("U" & Bonus_Buy_Row_Index).Value2 = Replace(Bonus_Buy_Name, "KA_", "KA_CPS3_")

                            End If

                            Call Gen_F001(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Sale_Unit)
                            Bonus_Buy_Index = Bonus_Buy_Index + 1
                        End If

                        Call Gen_F002(Mechanic, M_Number_Index, Bonus_Buy_Row_Index, Material_Number, New_Price, Discount_Amount, Discount_Percent, Bonus_Buy_Index, is_Mat_Group, False, Online_EN, Online_TH, Plant_Str, Mat_Group_Number_List, Get_Item_Index, Sale_Unit)

                        End If

                    End If
                End If
                If Mechanic <> "B1G1 (On Pack)" And Mechanic <> "B2G1 (On Pack)" Then
                    M_Number_Index = M_Number_Index + 1
                End If
            Next M_Number
        End If
        BBY_Sheet.Activate
         If Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P011" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P001" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P015" And Left(Profile_type, 4) <> "P103" Then
            Call Fill_Card_Type
        End If
        activebookname = ActiveWorkbook.Name
        Application.Run "Convert_C_to_AB_Template_V2.xlsb!Save_File_MER_C", KA_File_Name
        Call Add_Data_to_SQL(tmp_path_C2AB_Template, KA_File_Name)
        Call Copy_File_To_Approve_Folder(KA_File_Name)
        Set Save_book = Workbooks.Open(KA_File_Name)
        Application.DisplayAlerts = False
        Save_book.Close
        Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub Copy_From_Input_to_STD(wb_MerC As Workbook, sheet_MerC As Worksheet, std_Book As Workbook, std_C_Sheet As Worksheet, row_no_firstPromoItem As Integer, row_no_lastPromoItem As Integer, Promo_start_date As Date, Date_Add As Integer, Promotion_Name As String, Online_EN As String, Online_TH As String, WBS As String, Promo_start_time As String, Promo_end_time As String, All_day As String, Mon As String, Tue As String, Wed As String, Thu As String, Fri As String, Sat As String, Sun As String, Theme As String, Promo_end_date As Date)
    Dim Start_Day As String
    Dim Start_Month As String
    Dim Promo_start_year As String
    Dim End_Day As String
    Dim End_Month As String
    Dim Promo_end_year As String
    Dim Prepared_By As String
    
    sheet_MerC.Activate
    
     If Application.International(xlDateOrder) = 0 Then 'Month-Day-Year
        Promo_start_date = CDate(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value & " 23:59:59")
        Promo_end_date = CDate(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_month).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_day).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_year).Value & " 23:59:59")
    ElseIf Application.International(xlDateOrder) = 1 Then 'Day-Month_year
        Promo_start_date = CDate(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value & " 23:59:59")
        Promo_end_date = CDate(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_day).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_month).Value & "/" & sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_year).Value & " 23:59:59")
    End If
    
    'Promo_start_date = CDate(CInt(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value2) & "/" & CInt(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value2) & "/" & CInt(sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value2))
    sheet_MerC.Range("B" & CStr(row_no_firstPromoItem) & ":EQ" & CStr(row_no_lastPromoItem)).Copy Destination:=std_Book.Sheets(C2AB_sheet_name_Mer_C_Template).Range("B19") 'Change from EH to EI 11-07-2024
    Prepared_By = sheet_MerC.Range(cell_addr_Prepared_by).Value2
    
    
    
    std_C_Sheet.Activate
    'std_C_Sheet.Range("B" & CInt(row_start)).PasteSpecial Paste:=xlPastevalue2s
    'std_C_Sheet.Range("B" & CStr(row_no_firstPromoItem) & "DW" & CStr(row_no_lastPromoItem)).value2 = sheet_MerC.Range("B" & CStr(row_no_firstPromoItem) & ":DW" & CStr(row_no_lastPromoItem)).value2
    'Add day to generate normal promotion
    'Promo_start_date = DateAdd("d", Date_Add, Promo_start_date)
    Start_Day = Day(Promo_start_date)
    Start_Month = Month(Promo_start_date)
    Promo_start_year = IIf(Year(Promo_start_date) < 2000, Year(Promo_start_date) + 543, Year(Promo_start_date))
    
    If Promo_start_year < 2000 Then
        Promo_start_year = Promo_start_year + 543
    End If
    
    End_Day = Day(Promo_end_date)
    End_Month = Month(Promo_end_date)
    Promo_end_year = Year(Promo_end_date)
    
    If Promo_end_year < 2000 Then
        Promo_end_year = Promo_end_year + 543
    End If
    
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_day).Value2 = Start_Day
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_month).Value2 = Start_Month
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_year).Value2 = Promo_start_year
    
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_day).Value2 = End_Day
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_month).Value2 = End_Month
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_year).Value2 = Promo_end_year

    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_Name).Value2 = Promotion_Name
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_WBS_no).Value2 = WBS
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_start_Time).Value2 = Promo_start_time
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Promo_end_Time).Value2 = Promo_end_time
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_All_Day).Value2 = All_day
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Mon).Value2 = Mon
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Tue).Value2 = Tue
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Wed).Value2 = Wed
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Thu).Value2 = Thu
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Fri).Value2 = Fri
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Sat).Value2 = Sat
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Sun).Value2 = Sun
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Theme).Value2 = Theme
    std_C_Sheet.Range(cell_addr_Prepared_by).Value2 = Prepared_By

End Sub

Private Function Get_Purchasing_Group_Full_Name(Purchasing_Group As String) As String
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim Sub_Con_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set Sub_Con_Sheet = Sheets("Sub_condition")
        'Get Purchasing Group Full Name
    Purchasing_Group = Left(Trim(Purchasing_Group), 3)
    Dim g As Integer
    For g = 3 To 129
        If Purchasing_Group = Sub_Con_Sheet.Range("CT" & g).Value2 Then
            Purchasing_Group = Sub_Con_Sheet.Range("CV" & g).Value2
            Exit For
        End If
    Next g
    Get_Purchasing_Group_Full_Name = Purchasing_Group
End Function

Private Function Get_Profile_Type_Full_Name(Profile_type As String) As String
    Dim std_Book As Workbook
    Dim Profile_Master_Sheet As Worksheet
    Set std_Book = Workbooks.Open(tmp_path_C2AB_Template)
    Set Profile_Master_Sheet = Sheets("ZRT0013_10 updated 11-Nov")

    Dim p As Integer
    For p = 21 To 78
        If Profile_type = Profile_Master_Sheet.Range("A" & p).Value2 Then
            Profile_type = Profile_Master_Sheet.Range("D" & p).Value2
            Exit For
        End If
    Next p
    Get_Profile_Type_Full_Name = Profile_type
End Function

Private Function is_Offline_And_Online(Row_Index_Start As Integer, Row_Index_End As Integer, std_C_Sheet As Worksheet) As Boolean
    Dim Online_Plant_Column As Integer
    
    Online_Plant_Column = CInt(col_no_plant_end)
    
    Dim Offline_Check As Boolean
    Dim Online_Check As Boolean
    Dim last_row As Long
    last_row = std_C_Sheet.Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row
    
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To last_row
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_IM_Reject)).Value2 <> "Reject" Then
            If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, Online_Plant_Column).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, Online_Plant_Column).Value2 = "x" Then
                Online_Check = True
                Exit For
            Else
                Online_Check = False
            End If
        Else
            Online_Check = False
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    
    Dim p As Integer
    Dim s As Integer
    
    For p = 2 To Online_Plant_Column - 1
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, CInt(col_no_IM_Reject)).Value2 <> "Reject" Then
            If std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), p).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), p).Value2 = "x" Then
                Offline_Check = True
                Exit For
            Else
                Offline_Check = False
            End If
        Else
            Offline_Check = False
        End If
    Next p
    
    If Offline_Check = True And Online_Check = True Then
        is_Offline_And_Online = True
    Else
        is_Offline_And_Online = False
    End If
End Function

Private Function is_Online_Only(std_C_Sheet As Worksheet) As Boolean
    Dim Online_Plant_Column As Integer
    Online_Plant_Column = CInt(col_no_plant_end)
    
    Dim last_row As Long
    last_row = std_C_Sheet.Cells(Rows.Count, 32).End(xlUp).Row
    Dim Offline_Check As Boolean
    Dim Online_Check As Boolean
    Dim curr_row_no As Integer
    For curr_row_no = CInt(row_start) To last_row
        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, Online_Plant_Column).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(curr_row_no, Online_Plant_Column).Value2 = "x" Then
            Online_Check = True
            Exit For
        Else
            Online_Check = False
        End If
    Next curr_row_no
    Dim p As Integer
    Dim s As Integer
    
    For p = 2 To Online_Plant_Column - 1

        If std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), p).Value2 = "X" Or std_C_Sheet.Cells(CInt(row_start), p).Value2 = "x" Then
            Offline_Check = True
            Exit For
        Else
            Offline_Check = False
        End If
    Next p
    If Offline_Check = False And Online_Check = True Then
        is_Online_Only = True
    Else
        is_Online_Only = False
    End If
End Function

Private Sub Add_Data_to_SQL(MER_C_File_Name As String, Split_File_Name As String)
    Dim connection As ADODB.connection
    Set connection = New ADODB.connection
    
    With connection
        .ConnectionString = "Provider=SQLOLEDB;Data Source=" & server_name & ";Initial Catalog=" & database_name & ";User ID=sa;Password=Mpt@1234;"
        .ConnectionTimeout = 10
        .Open
    End With
    If connection.State = 1 Then
        Debug.Print "Connected!"
    End If
    
    Dim MER_C_File As String
    Dim Split_File As String
    Dim MER_C_File_Path As String
    
    MER_C_File = fso.GetFileName(srcPath_file)
    Split_File = fso.GetFileName(Split_File_Name)
    MER_C_File_Path = Replace(srcPath_file, MER_C_File, "")
    
    Dim sqlQuery As String
    'sqlQuery = "Select * from Promotion_BBY_Mer_C_Approve_Log"
    
    sqlQuery = "Insert Into Promotion_MER_C_Combine (MER_C_File_Name,Split_File_Name,Combine_Status,Create_Bonus_Buy_Status,Portal_File_Name,Save_MER_C_File_Path) Select '" + MER_C_File + "','" + Split_File + "',0,0,'','" + MER_C_File_Path + "'"
    
    connection.Execute sqlQuery
    
End Sub

Private Sub Copy_File_To_Approve_Folder(Source_File_Name As String)
    Dim SFTP_File_Name As String
    SFTP_File_Name = path_SFTP_approved & fso.GetFileName(Source_File_Name)
    fso.CopyFile Source_File_Name, SFTP_File_Name, True
End Sub
